end

	--[[
		ForceAutoLoad API
		=================
		Позволяет автоматически сохранять и восстанавливать значения
		отдельных элементов (Slider, Toggle, Input, Dropdown, Colorpicker)
		при каждом запуске скрипта.

		Данные хранятся в отдельной папке FAutoLoad (не в /settings/),
		по одному файлу на флаг элемента.

		Параметры в конфигурации элемента:
		  ForceAutoLoad = true   -- включить автосохранение для этого элемента
		  FALoadDelay   = 1.5   -- задержка в секундах перед загрузкой при старте (default: 0)

		Методы MacLib:
		  MacLib:InitForceAutoLoad()          -- инициализирует папку FAutoLoad (вызывается автоматически при SetFolder)
		  MacLib:FALSave(flag, elementObj)    -- сохраняет текущее значение элемента (вызывается при изменении)
		  MacLib:FALLoad(flag, elementObj, delay) -- загружает сохранённое значение (вызывается при создании элемента)
		  MacLib:FALClear(flag)               -- удаляет файл автосохранения для флага

		Пример использования:
		  section:Slider({
		    Name = "Speed",
		    Min = 0, Max = 100, Default = 50,
		    ForceAutoLoad = true,
		    FALoadDelay = 0.5,
		    Callback = function(v) ... end
		  }, "SpeedFlag")
	]]

	MacLib._falFolder = nil  -- путь к папке FAutoLoad

	function MacLib:InitForceAutoLoad()
		if isStudio or not (isfolder and makefolder) then return end
		MacLib._falFolder = MacLib.Folder .. "/FAutoLoad"
		if not isfolder(MacLib._falFolder) then
			makefolder(MacLib._falFolder)
		end
	end

	function MacLib:FALSave(flag, elementObj)
		if isStudio or not (MacLib._falFolder and writefile) then return end
		if not isfolder(MacLib._falFolder) then
			makefolder(MacLib._falFolder)
		end
		local path = MacLib._falFolder .. "/" .. tostring(flag) .. ".syl"
		local class = elementObj and elementObj.Class
		local data = {}
		if class == "Slider" then
			data = { type = "Slider", value = tostring(elementObj:GetValue()) }
		elseif class == "Toggle" then
			data = { type = "Toggle", state = elementObj:GetState() }
		elseif class == "Input" then
			data = { type = "Input", text = elementObj:GetText() }
		elseif class == "Dropdown" then
			data = { type = "Dropdown", value = elementObj.Value }
		elseif class == "Colorpicker" then
			local c = elementObj.Color
			if c then
				data = { type = "Colorpicker", r = c.R, g = c.G, b = c.B, a = elementObj.Alpha or 0 }
			end
		elseif class == "Keybind" then
			-- FALSave for Keybind: saves binded key name + mobile button visible state
			local bind = elementObj.GetBind and elementObj:GetBind()
			local bindName = (typeof(bind) == "EnumItem" and bind.Name) or nil
			local falFlag = elementObj._falFlag
			local mbVis = (MacLib._keybindBtnVisible and falFlag and MacLib._keybindBtnVisible[falFlag]) or false
			data = { type = "Keybind", bind = bindName, mbVisible = mbVis }
		end
		local ok, encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, data)
		if ok then
			pcall(writefile, path, encoded)
		end
	end

	function MacLib:FALLoad(flag, elementObj, delay)
		if isStudio or not (MacLib._falFolder and isfile and readfile) then return end
		task.spawn(function()
			if delay and delay > 0 then
				task.wait(delay)
			end
			local path = MacLib._falFolder .. "/" .. tostring(flag) .. ".syl"
			if not isfile(path) then return end
			local ok, data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, readfile(path))
			if not ok or not data then return end
			local class = data.type
			pcall(function()
				if class == "Slider" and elementObj.Class == "Slider" then
					elementObj:UpdateValue(tonumber(data.value), true)
				elseif class == "Toggle" and elementObj.Class == "Toggle" then
					elementObj:UpdateState(data.state)
				elseif class == "Input" and elementObj.Class == "Input" then
					if elementObj.UpdateText then elementObj:UpdateText(data.text) end
				elseif class == "Dropdown" and elementObj.Class == "Dropdown" then
					if elementObj.UpdateSelection then elementObj:UpdateSelection(data.value) end
				elseif class == "Colorpicker" and elementObj.Class == "Colorpicker" then
					if data.r and elementObj.SetColor then
						elementObj:SetColor(Color3.new(data.r, data.g, data.b))
					end
				elseif class == "Keybind" and elementObj.Class == "Keybind" then
					-- Restore bind key
					if data.bind and elementObj.Bind then
						local ok2, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[data.bind] end)
						if ok2 and kc then elementObj:Bind(kc) end
					end
					-- Restore mobile button visibility
					if data.mbVisible ~= nil and elementObj.SetMobileButtonVisibility then
						task.defer(function()
							elementObj:SetMobileButtonVisibility(data.mbVisible)
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
	end

	function MacLib:FALClear(flag)
		if isStudio or not (MacLib._falFolder and isfile) then return end
		local path = MacLib._falFolder .. "/" .. tostring(flag) .. ".syl"
		if isfile(path) then
			pcall(function() delfile(path) end)
		end
	end


	--[[
		MacLib:FALSetData(flag, value)
		Сохраняет произвольные кастомные данные под ключом `flag` в папке FAutoLoad.
		Аналог MacLib:SetData(), но хранится в FAutoLoad/flag.syl.
		Позволяет разработчику сохранять значения без привязки к UI-элементу.

		MacLib:FALGetData(flag, default?)
		Читает ранее сохранённые кастомные данные. Возвращает `default` если не найдено.

		MacLib:FALLoadData(flag, callback, delay?, default?)
		Отложенный хелпер — ждёт `delay` секунд, затем читает данные и вызывает callback(value).
		Поведение полностью аналогично ForceAutoLoad у элементов.

		Пример использования:
		  MacLib:FALSetData("MySpeed", 75)
		  MacLib:FALLoadData("MySpeed", function(v)
		      print("Loaded speed:", v)  --> 75
		  end, 0.5, 50)
	]]
	function MacLib:FALSetData(flag, value)
		if isStudio then return false, "Studio mode" end
		if not (MacLib._falFolder and writefile) then return false, "Config system unavailable" end
		if not isfolder(MacLib._falFolder) then makefolder(MacLib._falFolder) end
		local path = MacLib._falFolder .. "/" .. tostring(flag) .. ".syl"
		local ok, encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, { type = "Custom", value = value })
		if not ok then return false, "Encode error: " .. tostring(encoded) end
		local wrote, err = pcall(writefile, path, encoded)
		return wrote, wrote and nil or tostring(err)
	end

	function MacLib:FALGetData(flag, default)
		if isStudio then return default end
		if not (MacLib._falFolder and isfile and readfile) then return default end
		local path = MacLib._falFolder .. "/" .. tostring(flag) .. ".syl"
		if not isfile(path) then return default end
		local ok, data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, readfile(path))
		if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or data.type ~= "Custom" then return default end
		return data.value ~= nil and data.value or default
	end

	function MacLib:FALLoadData(flag, callback, delay, default)
		task.spawn(function()
			if delay and delay > 0 then task.wait(delay) end
			local value = MacLib:FALGetData(flag, default)
			if callback then callback(value) end
		end)
	end


	--[[
		MacLib:Preloader(url, config)

		Загружает внешний Lua-модуль и регистрирует его как полноценный элемент
		библиотеки. Разработчик сам определяет методы и логику элемента.

		url    -- URL до raw Lua-скрипта (string)
		config -- таблица конфигурации:
		  {
		    Name    = "MyElement",   -- имя для регистрации (опционально)
		    Window  = WindowFunctions, -- окно для передачи в модуль
		    Timeout = 10,            -- таймаут загрузки в секундах (default: 10)
		    onLoad  = function(element) ... end,  -- вызывается после загрузки
		    onError = function(err)   ... end,    -- вызывается при ошибке
		  }

		Внешний модуль (url) должен возвращать функцию или таблицу:
		  - Если функция: вызывается с context-таблицей, результат = элемент
		  - Если таблица: возвращается напрямую как элемент

		Context-таблица, передаваемая в модуль:
		  {
		    MacLib  = MacLib,
		    Options = MacLib.Options,
		    Window  = config.Window,
		    Name    = config.Name,
		  }

		Пример внешнего модуля (MyElement.lua):
		  return function(ctx)
		    local element = {}
		    local _value = 0

		    -- Разработчик сам создаёт любые методы:
		    function element:SetValue(v) _value = v end
		    function element:GetValue() return _value end
		    function element:Build(section, settings)
		      -- создаём UI через section:Slider / section:Toggle и т.д.
		      section:Slider({ Name = settings.Name, Min=0, Max=100, Callback=function(v) _value=v end })
		    end

		    return element
		  end

		Пример использования:
		  MacLib:Preloader("https://.../MyElement.lua", {
		    Window = Window,
		    Name   = "MyElement",
		    onLoad = function(element)
		      element:Build(tab:Section({ Name = "Custom" }), { Name = "Speed" })
		      -- теперь можно вызывать: element:SetValue(50)
		    end,
		  })
	]]
	--[[
		MacLib:Preloader(moduleResult, config)

		Интегрирует уже загруженный внешний модуль в MacLib.
		Разработчик сам загружает файл через loadstring/loadfile/require,
		а Preloader только вызывает его с нужным контекстом.

		moduleResult -- результат loadstring(src)() или require(), т.е. функция или таблица
		config       -- таблица конфигурации (опционально):
		  {
		    Window  = Window,              -- передаётся в ctx.Window
		    Name    = "MyElement",         -- имя для логирования
		    onLoad  = function(el) end,    -- callback после успешного вызова
		    onError = function(err) end,   -- callback при ошибке
		  }

		Внешний модуль должен быть функцией(ctx) или таблицей.
		  - Функция: вызывается с ctx = { MacLib, Options, Window, Name }
		  - Таблица:  возвращается напрямую как элемент

		Пример:
		  local src = game:HttpGet("https://.../ProgressBar_Element.lua")
		  local moduleFn = loadstring(src)
		  MacLib:Preloader(moduleFn, {
		    Window  = Window,
		    Name    = "ProgressBar",
		    onLoad  = function() print("loaded!") end,
		  })

		Также поддерживает уже вызванный результат:
		  local mod = loadstring(src)()   -- вызвали сами
		  MacLib:Preloader(mod, { onLoad = function(el) ... end })

		Обратная совместимость:
		  MacLib:Preloader(fn, callback)  -- callback = onLoad
	]]
	MacLib._loadedModules   = MacLib._loadedModules   or {}
	MacLib._onLoadCallbacks = MacLib._onLoadCallbacks or {}

	function MacLib:OnLoad(name, callback)
		if MacLib._loadedModules[name] then
			task.spawn(callback)
		else
			MacLib._onLoadCallbacks[name] = MacLib._onLoadCallbacks[name] or {}
			table.insert(MacLib._onLoadCallbacks[name], callback)
		end
	end

	function MacLib:IsLoaded(name)
		return MacLib._loadedModules[name] == true
	end

		function MacLib:Preloader(moduleResult, config)
		-- Поддержка старого API: Preloader(fn, callback)
		if type(config) == "function" then
			config = { onLoad = config }
		end
		config = config or {}

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				local ctx = {
					MacLib  = MacLib,
					Options = MacLib.Options,
					Window  = config.Window,
					Name    = config.Name,
				}

				local mod = moduleResult

				-- Если передана функция — вызываем с ctx
				if type(mod) == "function" then
					mod = mod(ctx)
				end

				-- Если результат тоже функция (loadstring вернул closure) — вызываем ещё раз
				if type(mod) == "function" then
					mod = mod(ctx)
				end

				return mod
			end)

			if ok then
				if type(config.onLoad) == "function" then
					config.onLoad(result)
				end
				-- FIX: помечаем модуль загруженным и диспатчим MacLib:OnLoad callbacks
				if config.Name then
					MacLib._loadedModules[config.Name] = true
					local cbs = MacLib._onLoadCallbacks and MacLib._onLoadCallbacks[config.Name]
					if cbs then
						for _, cb in ipairs(cbs) do
							task.spawn(cb)
						end
						MacLib._onLoadCallbacks[config.Name] = nil
					end
				end
			else
				warn("[MacLib:Preloader] Error" .. (config.Name and (" in '" .. config.Name .. "'") or "") .. ": " .. tostring(result))
				if type(config.onError) == "function" then
					config.onError(result)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ============================================================
	-- V11 EXTENDED API
	-- ============================================================

	--[[
		MacLib:Extend(name, fn)
		Добавляет (или заменяет) метод прямо в таблицу MacLib.
		Используется внешними модулями, чтобы патчить MacLib так же,
		как это делает KeySystem (MacLib:KeySystem = ...).
		Аналог Object.assign / prototype extension.

		Пример:
		  MacLib:Extend("KeySystem", function(self, cfg)
		    -- build overlay, return { Show, Hide, Destroy, SetKey }
		  end)
		  local ks = MacLib:KeySystem({...})
	]]
	function MacLib:Extend(name, fn)
		assert(type(name) == "string", "[MacLib:Extend] name must be a string")
		assert(type(fn) == "function", "[MacLib:Extend] fn must be a function")
		MacLib[name] = function(self, ...)
			return fn(self, ...)
		end
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:ExtendSection(name, fn)
		Алиас / обёртка над MacLib:PatchSection.
		Добавляет метод во все существующие и будущие секции.
		Предпочтительный API для расширений вместо прямого PatchSection.

		Пример:
		  MacLib:ExtendSection("ProgressBar", function(self, settings, flag)
		    return MacLib:CreateCustomElement(self, "ProgressBar", settings, flag)
		  end)
		  section:ProgressBar({ Name = "Load", Default = 0 }, "PB")
	]]
	function MacLib:ExtendSection(name, fn)
		assert(type(name) == "string", "[MacLib:ExtendSection] name must be a string")
		assert(type(fn) == "function", "[MacLib:ExtendSection] fn must be a function")
		MacLib:PatchSection(name, fn)
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:Hook(name, hookFn)
		Перехватывает существующий метод MacLib.
		hookFn получает (original, ...) — original это исходная функция.
		Позволяет реализовывать pre/post-логику без разрушения оригинала.

		Пример (post-hook — логировать каждый Notify):
		  MacLib:Hook("Notify", function(original, self, settings)
		    settings.Title = "[Hooked] " .. (settings.Title or "")
		    return original(self, settings)
		  end)

		Пример (pre-hook — блокировать пустые уведомления):
		  MacLib:Hook("Notify", function(original, self, settings)
		    if not settings.Description or settings.Description == "" then return end
		    return original(self, settings)
		  end)
	]]
	function MacLib:Hook(name, hookFn)
		assert(type(name) == "string",   "[MacLib:Hook] name must be a string")
		assert(type(hookFn) == "function", "[MacLib:Hook] hookFn must be a function")
		local original = MacLib[name]
		assert(type(original) == "function",
			"[MacLib:Hook] '" .. name .. "' is not an existing MacLib method — cannot hook")
		MacLib[name] = function(self, ...)
			return hookFn(original, self, ...)
		end
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:WatchOption(flag, fn)
		Подписывается на изменение значения элемента по флагу,
		НЕ заменяя исходный Callback. Все watcher-ы вызываются
		после оригинального Callback через task.spawn.

		fn(value, alpha?) — сигнатура совпадает с Callback элемента.

		Поддерживаемые типы: Toggle, Slider, Input, Dropdown, Colorpicker.
		Для остальных типов watcher не будет вызван.

		Возвращает функцию disconnect(), которая снимает watcher.

		Пример:
		  local stop = MacLib:WatchOption("SpeedFlag", function(v)
		    print("Speed changed to", v)
		  end)
		  -- позже:
		  stop()
	]]
	MacLib._watchers = MacLib._watchers or {}

	function MacLib:WatchOption(flag, fn)
		assert(type(flag) == "string",  "[MacLib:WatchOption] flag must be a string")
		assert(type(fn)   == "function","[MacLib:WatchOption] fn must be a function")

		MacLib._watchers[flag] = MacLib._watchers[flag] or {}
		local id = {} -- unique key
		MacLib._watchers[flag][id] = fn

		local opt = MacLib.Options[flag]
		if opt and not opt._watcherPatched then
			opt._watcherPatched = true
			local origCB = opt.Settings and opt.Settings.Callback
			if opt.Settings then
				opt.Settings.Callback = function(...)
					local args = {...}
					if origCB then origCB(table.unpack(args)) end
					local watchers = MacLib._watchers[flag]
					if watchers then
						for _, wfn in next, watchers do
							task.spawn(wfn, table.unpack(args))
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return function()
			if MacLib._watchers[flag] then
				MacLib._watchers[flag][id] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:BatchSet(tbl)
		Массово применяет значения к flagged-элементам.
		tbl — таблица вида { [flag] = value, ... }.
		Использует UpdateState / UpdateValue / UpdateSelection /
		UpdateText / SetColor в зависимости от класса элемента.
		silent = true предотвращает вызов Callback при установке.

		Пример:
		  MacLib:BatchSet({
		    Enabled  = true,      -- Toggle
		    Speed    = 75,        -- Slider
		    Fruit    = "Mango",   -- Dropdown
		    Nickname = "Player",  -- Input
		  })
	]]
	function MacLib:BatchSet(tbl, silent)
		assert(type(tbl) == "table", "[MacLib:BatchSet] tbl must be a table")
		for flag, value in next, tbl do
			local opt = MacLib.Options[flag]
			if not opt then continue end
			local class = opt.Class
			local ok = pcall(function()
				if class == "Toggle" then
					opt:UpdateState(value, silent)
				elseif class == "Slider" then
					opt:UpdateValue(value, silent)
				elseif class == "Dropdown" then
					opt:UpdateSelection(value)
				elseif class == "Input" then
					if opt.UpdateText then opt:UpdateText(value)
					elseif opt.GetInput then opt.Settings.Placeholder = value end
				elseif class == "Colorpicker" then
					if type(value) == "table" then
						if value.color then opt:SetColor(value.color) end
						if value.alpha ~= nil then opt:SetAlpha(value.alpha) end
					elseif typeof(value) == "Color3" then
						opt:SetColor(value)
					end
				end
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("[MacLib:BatchSet] Failed to set flag '" .. tostring(flag) .. "'")
			end
		end
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:RemoveOption(flag)
		Удаляет элемент из реестра MacLib.Options.
		Если у элемента есть поле .frame или .rootFrame — уничтожает UI.
		Также снимает все WatchOption-подписки для этого флага.

		Возвращает true если элемент был найден и удалён, иначе false.

		Пример:
		  MacLib:RemoveOption("MyToggle")
	]]
	function MacLib:RemoveOption(flag)
		assert(type(flag) == "string", "[MacLib:RemoveOption] flag must be a string")
		local opt = MacLib.Options[flag]
		if not opt then return false end

		-- Уничтожить UI если есть rootFrame / frame
		local frame = opt.frame or opt.rootFrame
		if frame and typeof(frame) == "Instance" and frame.Parent then
			pcall(function() frame:Destroy() end)
		end

		-- Снять watchers
		if MacLib._watchers and MacLib._watchers[flag] then
			MacLib._watchers[flag] = nil
		end

		-- Удалить из реестра
		MacLib.Options[flag] = nil
		return true
	end

	--[[
		MacLib:GetOptions()
		Возвращает копию таблицы всех flagged-элементов (MacLib.Options).
		Безопасна для итерации — изменение результата не влияет на реестр.

		Пример:
		  local opts = MacLib:GetOptions()
		  for flag, element in next, opts do
		    print(flag, element.Class)
		  end
	]]
	function MacLib:GetOptions()
		local copy = {}
		for k, v in next, MacLib.Options do
			copy[k] = v
		end
		return copy
	end

	-- ============================================================
	-- END V11 EXTENDED API
	-- ============================================================

return MacLib
end)()

--[[
    Syllinse shell + embedded MacLib V2 controls
    Keeps the Syllinse icon rail, grayscale theme, search, branding and callback wiring.
    Mobile layouts fit the viewport and stack the native MacLib columns on narrow screens.
    MacLib's source is embedded below, so no remote library URL fetch is required.

    Run in the same Roblox client environment as your existing Syllinse UI.
    The shell's default toggle key is RightControl and can be changed in Settings.
]]


local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local showLauncherButton =
    UserInputService.TouchEnabled
    and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

-- Forward declaration for the MacLib compatibility adapter used by Syllinse callbacks.
local Lumen

-- LOGO: use the updated Roblox image asset ID supplied for Syllinse.
local LOGO_IMAGE_ID = "rbxassetid://123721229450612"
local logoImageSource = LOGO_IMAGE_ID

local existing = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SyllinseUI")
if existing then
    existing:Destroy()
end

-- Use the same native BuilderSans family as MacLib. FontFace avoids the mismatched
-- legacy enum-font substitutions that made spacing/letter shapes look inconsistent.
local FONT_REGULAR = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
local FONT_MEDIUM = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.Medium)
local FONT_SEMIBOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/BuilderSans.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)

local Theme = {
    -- Warm charcoal palette with clearer, neutral grayscale text.
    Root = Color3.fromRGB(23, 22, 22),
    Surface = Color3.fromRGB(26, 25, 25),
    SurfaceHover = Color3.fromRGB(34, 33, 33),
    Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(43, 42, 42),
    Divider = Color3.fromRGB(40, 39, 39),
    -- Restore the original Syllinse grayscale palette; readable without turning the
    -- headings and controls into bright white text.
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223),
    PanelText = Color3.fromRGB(139, 137, 137),
    -- Brighter neutral gray for section headers, matched by their header icons.
    PanelTitle = Color3.fromRGB(170, 168, 168),
    -- Roblox image assets render darker than opaque text at the same RGB tint;
    -- slightly raise the active header icon tint so its perceived brightness matches the title.
    HeaderIconTint = Color3.fromRGB(210, 208, 208),
    PanelSecondary = Color3.fromRGB(116, 114, 114),
    PanelIcon = Color3.fromRGB(170, 168, 168),
    PanelValue = Color3.fromRGB(128, 126, 126),
    SliderTrack = Color3.fromRGB(64, 62, 62),
    SliderHead = Color3.fromRGB(112, 110, 110),
    SliderHandle = Color3.fromRGB(194, 192, 192),
    Muted = Color3.fromRGB(139, 137, 137),
    Dim = Color3.fromRGB(87, 85, 85),
    Accent = Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223),
    IconActive = Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223),
    IconIdle = Color3.fromRGB(139, 137, 137),
    -- A restrained hover gray: perceptibly brighter than idle, without looking white.
    -- Use the exact same tint for hovered icons and labels so they read as one control.
    IconHover = Color3.fromRGB(184, 182, 182),
}

-- Opaque text glyphs look brighter than tinted icon artwork at the same RGB value.
-- Blend terminology text tints back toward the charcoal root so they visually match the icons,
-- including when the user changes the active accent color.
local function terminologyTint(iconTint, strength)
    local base = Theme.Root
    return Color3.new(
        base.R + (iconTint.R - base.R) * strength,
        base.G + (iconTint.G - base.G) * strength,
        base.B + (iconTint.B - base.B) * strength
    )
end

local function addCorner(instance, radius)
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    corner.Parent = instance
    return corner
end

local function addStroke(instance, color, transparency, thickness)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = color or Theme.Stroke
    stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0
    stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent = instance
    return stroke
end

local function makeFrame(name, parent, size, position, color, transparency)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = name
    frame.Size = size
    frame.Position = position
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Surface
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = transparency or 0
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = parent
    return frame
end

local function makeText(name, parent, text, size, position, fontSize, color, font, alignment)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = name
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Text = text
    label.Size = size
    label.Position = position
    label.FontFace = font or FONT_REGULAR
    label.TextScaled = false
    label.RichText = false
    label.AutoLocalize = false
    label.TextSize = fontSize or 14
    label.TextColor3 = color or Theme.Text
    label.TextXAlignment = alignment or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    label.Parent = parent
    return label
end

local function tween(instance, duration, properties)
    local animation = TweenService:Create(
        instance,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        properties
    )
    animation:Play()
    return animation
end

-- Screen GUI
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "SyllinseUI"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.DisplayOrder = 30
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
gui.Parent = playerGui

local root = makeFrame(
    "Window",
    gui,
    UDim2.fromOffset(940, 650),
    UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
    Theme.Root
)
root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
root.ClipsDescendants = true
addCorner(root, 18)
local shellStroke = addStroke(root, Theme.Stroke, 0.12, 1)

local uiScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
uiScale.Name = "ResponsiveScale"
uiScale.Parent = root

-- Keep one UIScale and combine its viewport fit with the motion factor. This avoids
-- competing UIScale modifiers while allowing the same smooth scale morph on all screens.
local responsiveScaleValue = 1
local baseWindowSize -- refreshed whenever the viewport changes
local layoutResponsiveShell -- assigned after the shell's header/footer controls are constructed
local compactTouchLayout = false
local windowMotionFactor = Instance.new("NumberValue")
windowMotionFactor.Name = "WindowMotionFactor"
windowMotionFactor.Value = 0.90
windowMotionFactor.Changed:Connect(function(value)
    uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue * value
end)
root.Visible = false -- shown by the initial Syllinse-branded opening transition near the end

-- Use viewport-fit sizing on touch devices instead of shrinking every control to tiny pixels.
-- Desktop retains the 940x650 canvas and its familiar proportional scale.
local function updateScale()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    if showLauncherButton then
        local fittedWidth = math.min(940, math.max(320, viewport.X - 16))
        local fittedHeight = math.min(650, math.max(320, viewport.Y - 20))
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(fittedWidth, fittedHeight)
        responsiveScaleValue = 1
    else
        local fittedScale = math.min(viewport.X / 960, viewport.Y / 684)
        responsiveScaleValue = math.clamp(fittedScale, 0.55, 1)
        root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(940, 650)
    end
    if baseWindowSize then baseWindowSize = root.Size end
    uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue * windowMotionFactor.Value
    if layoutResponsiveShell then task.defer(layoutResponsiveShell) end
end

updateScale()
if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
    Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateScale)
end

addCorner(sidebar, 18) -- Prevent the sidebar fill from squaring off the outer window corners.
local sideDivider = makeFrame("SidebarDivider", root, UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(76, 0), Theme.Divider)
sideDivider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15

local activeName = "Combat"
local currentSearchMode = "none" -- Declared here so sidebar clicks can respect live feature searches.
local navButtons = {}

local showMenuFunctionTerminology = false
local terminologyLabels = {}

local lumenPages = {}

local headerIcons = {} -- Cached per-category header icons: no switching-time image recreation/flicker.

local searchBox

-- Each icon has a vector fallback, hidden while the real Roblox asset preloads. The real icon
-- appears only after a successful fetch, so a generic fallback never flashes before the user's icon.
-- If Roblox rejects the image, clear the broken-image placeholder and reveal the vector fallback.
local ICON_KIND_BY_ID = {
    ["123721229450612"] = "Logo",
    ["97863600700870"] = "Combat",
    ["128100651128415"] = "Desync",
    ["76957546191785"] = "Visuals",
    ["130068885175166"] = "Misc",
    ["108550744861024"] = "Configs",
    ["76254758372811"] = "Settings",
}
local warnedAssetIds = {}

local function makeVectorFallback(name, parent, size, position, anchorPoint, zIndex, kind)
    size = size or 24
    local rootFallback = Instance.new("Frame")
    rootFallback.Name = name or "ImageFallback"
    rootFallback.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
    rootFallback.Position = position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    rootFallback.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    rootFallback.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rootFallback.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rootFallback.ZIndex = zIndex or 2
    rootFallback.Parent = parent

    local color = Theme.Muted
    local function line(x1, y1, x2, y2, thickness)
        local scale = size / 24
        local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
        local piece = Instance.new("Frame")
        piece.Name = "Stroke"
        piece.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        piece.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(((x1 + x2) / 2) * scale, ((y1 + y2) / 2) * scale)
        piece.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * scale), math.max(1, thickness * scale))
        piece.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
        piece.BackgroundColor3 = color
        piece.BorderSizePixel = 0
        piece.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 1
        piece.Parent = rootFallback
        addCorner(piece, math.max(1, math.floor(thickness * scale)))
        return piece
    end
    local function dot(x, y, diameter, fill)
        local scale = size / 24
        local c = Instance.new("Frame")
        c.Name = "Dot"
        c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        c.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(diameter * scale, diameter * scale)
        c.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * scale, y * scale)
        c.BackgroundColor3 = fill or color
        c.BorderSizePixel = 0
        c.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 2
        c.Parent = rootFallback
        addCorner(c, math.ceil(diameter * scale))
        return c
    end

    if kind == "Logo" then
        local letter = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        letter.Name = "LogoFallbackS"
        letter.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        letter.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        letter.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
        letter.FontFace = FONT_SEMIBOLD
        letter.Text = "S"
        letter.TextSize = size * 0.9
        letter.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        letter.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45
        letter.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 33, 33)
        letter.ZIndex = rootFallback.ZIndex + 1
        letter.Parent = rootFallback
        local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(0, 238, 214)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.28, Color3.fromRGB(0, 177, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, Color3.fromRGB(105, 77, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, Color3.fromRGB(239, 54, 211)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 242, 55)),
        })
        gradient.Rotation = -28
        gradient.Parent = letter
        return rootFallback
    elseif kind == "Combat" then
        -- Two crossed blades with short hilts; neutral monochrome until the source image loads.
        line(5, 4, 19, 20, 2.1)
        line(19, 4, 5, 20, 2.1)
        line(4, 3, 8, 7, 2.6)
        line(16, 17, 20, 21, 2.6)
        line(16, 3, 20, 7, 2.6)
        line(4, 17, 8, 21, 2.6)
    elseif kind == "Desync" then
        line(5, 4, 19, 4, 1.8)
        line(5, 4, 5, 13, 1.8)
        line(19, 4, 19, 13, 1.8)
        line(5, 13, 8, 17, 1.8)
        line(19, 13, 16, 17, 1.8)
        line(8, 17, 12, 20, 1.8)
        line(16, 17, 12, 20, 1.8)
    elseif kind == "Visuals" then
        line(3, 12, 8, 7, 1.7)
        line(8, 7, 16, 7, 1.7)
        line(16, 7, 21, 12, 1.7)
        line(21, 12, 16, 17, 1.7)
        line(16, 17, 8, 17, 1.7)
        line(8, 17, 3, 12, 1.7)
        dot(12, 12, 5.2, color)
    elseif kind == "Misc" then
        line(7, 6, 20, 6, 1.8)
        line(7, 12, 20, 12, 1.8)
        line(7, 18, 20, 18, 1.8)
        dot(4, 6, 2.2, color)
        dot(4, 12, 2.2, color)
        dot(4, 18, 2.2, color)
    elseif kind == "Configs" then
        line(6, 3, 15, 3, 1.5)
        line(15, 3, 19, 7, 1.5)
        line(19, 7, 19, 21, 1.5)
        line(19, 21, 6, 21, 1.5)
        line(6, 21, 6, 3, 1.5)
        line(15, 3, 15, 7, 1.5)
        line(15, 7, 19, 7, 1.5)
        line(9, 11, 16, 11, 1.5)
        line(9, 14, 16, 14, 1.5)
        line(9, 17, 16, 17, 1.5)
    else
        -- Generic fallback for any other image-based Lumen feature entry.
        line(5, 7, 19, 7, 1.8)
        line(5, 12, 19, 12, 1.8)
        line(5, 17, 19, 17, 1.8)
    end
    return rootFallback
end

local function recolorFallback(fallback, active, activeColor, idleColor)
    if not fallback then return end
    local color = active and (activeColor or Theme.IconActive) or (idleColor or Theme.IconIdle)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(fallback:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("Frame") then
            descendant.BackgroundColor3 = color
            descendant.BackgroundTransparency = active and 0 or 0.04
        elseif descendant:IsA("TextLabel") then
            descendant.TextColor3 = color
            descendant.TextTransparency = 0
        elseif descendant:IsA("UIStroke") then
            descendant.Color = color
            descendant.Transparency = active and 0 or 0.04
        end
    end
end

local function setRobloxImage(imageLabel, assetUri, _thumbSize, fallback)
    if not imageLabel or not assetUri or assetUri == "" then
        return
    end
    if not fallback and imageLabel.Parent then
        fallback = imageLabel.Parent:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
    end

    local source = tostring(assetUri)
    local assetId = source:match("(%d+)")
    local contentUri
    if source:match("^%s*rbxassetid://") then
        contentUri = source:gsub("^%s+", "")
    elseif source:match("^%s*%d+%s*$") then
        contentUri = "rbxassetid://" .. source:match("%d+")
    else
        contentUri = source
    end

    if assetId then imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseAssetId", assetId) end
    imageLabel.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0
    imageLabel.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    imageLabel.Visible = false
    if fallback then fallback.Visible = false end

    task.spawn(function()
        local function tryLoad(uri)
            local success = false
            local statusText = "no fetch callback status"
            imageLabel.Visible = false
            imageLabel.Image = uri
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({ imageLabel }, function(_contentId, fetchStatus)
                    statusText = tostring(fetchStatus)
                    if fetchStatus == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then
                        success = true
                    end
                end)
            end)
            if not ok then
                return false, statusText, err
            end
            return success, statusText, nil
        end

        local loaded, status, err = tryLoad(contentUri)
        local usedThumbnail = false

        -- Only try a thumbnail if the direct image content failed. Keep the image hidden
        -- while loading so neither a broken-image placeholder nor a temporary generic icon flashes.
        if not loaded and assetId then
            imageLabel.Image = ""
            task.wait(0.1)
            local thumbUri = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. assetId .. "&w=420&h=420"
            local thumbLoaded, thumbStatus, thumbErr = tryLoad(thumbUri)
            if thumbLoaded then
                loaded = true
                usedThumbnail = true
            else
                status = thumbStatus or status
                err = thumbErr or err
            end
        end

        if not imageLabel.Parent then return end
        if loaded then
            imageLabel.Visible = true
            if fallback then fallback.Visible = false end
            if usedThumbnail then
                imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseLoadedAsThumbnail", true)
            end
            return
        end

        imageLabel.Image = ""
        imageLabel.Visible = false
        imageLabel:SetAttribute("SyllinseImageLoadFailed", true)
        if fallback then
            -- A fallback can fail after the current tab was selected; style it from the
            -- current selection immediately instead of waiting for the next tab click.
            local parentName = imageLabel.Parent and imageLabel.Parent.Name or ""
            local selected = parentName == (activeName .. "Button")
                or parentName == ("HeaderIconHolder_" .. activeName)
            local isLogo = assetId == LOGO_IMAGE_ID:match("(%d+)")
            if not isLogo then
                recolorFallback(fallback, selected)
            end
            fallback.Visible = true
        end
        local warningId = assetId or contentUri
        if not warnedAssetIds[warningId] then
            warnedAssetIds[warningId] = true
            if err then
                warn("[Syllinse] Could not preload image " .. tostring(warningId) .. ": " .. tostring(err) .. ". Keeping the vector fallback visible.")
            else
                warn("[Syllinse] Roblox could not fetch image asset " .. tostring(warningId) ..
                    " (" .. tostring(status) .. "). Keeping the vector fallback visible. Check the content ID and experience permissions.")
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Create an image icon plus a drawn fallback. Broken Roblox image placeholders are never shown.
local function makeImageIcon(name, parent, assetUri, size, position, anchorPoint, zIndex, _color, fallbackSize, imageWidth, imageHeight)
    local id = tostring(assetUri or ""):match("(%d+)")
    local kind = ICON_KIND_BY_ID[id] or name or "Generic"
    local fallback = makeVectorFallback(
        "ImageFallback", parent, fallbackSize or size or 24, position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
        anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0), zIndex or 2, kind
    )

    local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    image.Name = name or "Icon"
    image.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(imageWidth or size or 24, imageHeight or size or 24)
    image.Position = position or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
    image.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    image.BorderSizePixel = 0
    image.Image = ""
    image.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    image.ImageTransparency = 0
    image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    image.ZIndex = (zIndex or 2) + 1
    image.Visible = false
    image.Parent = parent
    setRobloxImage(image, assetUri, nil, fallback)
    image:SetAttribute("SyllinseFallbackName", fallback.Name)
    return image
end

-- Keep a colorful S visible until Roblox confirms the real logo image can be loaded.
local logoFallback = makeVectorFallback(
    "ImageFallback", sidebar, 60, UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10), Vector2.new(0, 0), 2, "Logo"
)
-- If the execution environment supports local custom assets and the PNG is in its working
-- directory, prefer the supplied original logo PNG. Otherwise use the Roblox asset ID.
-- This is optional and never blocks the UI from opening.
local function findLocalLogoAsset()
    local customAsset = (type(getcustomasset) == "function" and getcustomasset)
        or (type(getsynasset) == "function" and getsynasset)
    if type(customAsset) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end
    local paths = { "Syllinse_Logo.png", "Syllinse/Syllinse_Logo.png", "assets/Syllinse_Logo.png" }
    for _, path in ipairs(paths) do
        local okFile, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
        if okFile and exists then
            local okAsset, content = pcall(customAsset, path)
            if okAsset and type(content) == "string" and content ~= "" then
                return content
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

logoImageSource = findLocalLogoAsset() or logoImageSource
setRobloxImage(logo, logoImageSource, nil, logoFallback)

-- Load the updated Syllinse logo asset ID and the updated icon asset IDs without converting
-- them to thumbnail URLs.
-- A small neutral vector gear for Settings because no Settings asset ID was supplied.
-- It is drawn from GUI primitives rather than a font glyph, avoiding missing-box icons.
local function makeGearIcon(parent, size, position, color, zIndex, anchorPoint)
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = "GearIcon"
    container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
    container.Position = position
    container.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.BorderSizePixel = 0
    container.ZIndex = zIndex or 3
    container.Parent = parent

    local parts = {}
    local center = size / 2
    local radius = size * 0.33
    local toothW = math.max(3, math.floor(size * 0.19))
    local toothH = math.max(4, math.floor(size * 0.28))
    for i = 0, 7 do
        local a = (math.pi * 2 * i / 8) - math.pi / 2
        local tooth = Instance.new("Frame")
        tooth.Name = "Tooth" .. tostring(i + 1)
        tooth.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        tooth.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(toothW, toothH)
        tooth.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(center + math.cos(a) * radius, center + math.sin(a) * radius)
        tooth.Rotation = i * 45
        tooth.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Muted
        tooth.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tooth.ZIndex = container.ZIndex
        tooth.Parent = container
        addCorner(tooth, 1)
        table.insert(parts, tooth)
    end

    local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
    ring.Name = "GearRing"
    ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.56, size * 0.56)
    ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
    ring.ZIndex = container.ZIndex + 1
    ring.Parent = container
    addCorner(ring, size)
    local ringStroke = addStroke(ring, color or Theme.Muted, 0, 1.5)

    local centerDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    centerDot.Name = "GearCenter"
    centerDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    centerDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(3, size * 0.18), math.max(3, size * 0.18))
    centerDot.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    centerDot.BackgroundColor3 = color or Theme.Muted
    centerDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    centerDot.ZIndex = container.ZIndex + 2
    centerDot.Parent = container
    addCorner(centerDot, size)

    return { Container = container, Parts = parts, RingStroke = ringStroke, Center = centerDot }
end

local function setGearIconColor(gear, color)
    if not gear then return end
    for _, part in ipairs(gear.Parts) do
        part.BackgroundColor3 = color
    end
    gear.RingStroke.Color = color
    gear.Center.BackgroundColor3 = color
end

local function makeSearchGlyph(parent)
    -- Conventional magnifying glass. The handle's inner endpoint meets the outside
    -- of the lens at 45 degrees; the lens is drawn above the joint so no line crosses it.
    local handle = Instance.new("Frame")
    handle.Name = "SearchHandle"
    handle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    handle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7.5, 1.5)
    handle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26.4, 28.4)
    handle.Rotation = 45
    handle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 203, 203)
    handle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    handle.ZIndex = 7
    handle.Parent = parent
    addCorner(handle, 2)

    local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
    circle.Name = "SearchLens"
    circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
    circle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13.5, 15.5)
    circle.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    circle.BackgroundTransparency = 0 -- masks the handle beneath the lens interior
    circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    circle.ZIndex = 8
    circle.Parent = parent
    addCorner(circle, 20)
    addStroke(circle, Color3.fromRGB(205, 203, 203), 0.02, 1.25)
    return circle
end

local function updateHeaderIcon(name)
    for tabName, entry in pairs(headerIcons) do
        local selected = (tabName == name)
        entry.Holder.Visible = selected
        if entry.Icon then
            -- Match the active category icon to the brighter gray header title.
            entry.Icon.ImageColor3 = selected and Theme.HeaderIconTint or Theme.PanelIcon
            entry.Icon.ImageTransparency = 0
        end
        recolorFallback(
            entry.Holder:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback"),
            selected,
            Theme.PanelTitle,
            Theme.PanelIcon
        )
    end
end

local function setFallbackEmphasis(parent, active)
    local fallback = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
    recolorFallback(fallback, active)
end

local function setActiveTab(name)
    activeName = name
    local found
    for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
        if item.Name == name then
            found = item
        end
    end

    if headerTitle then
        headerTitle.Text = name
        -- Keep a small, consistent gap from the visible icon; set per category in navItems.
        headerTitle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(found and found.HeaderTitleX or 51, 19)
    end
    updateHeaderIcon(name)
    if headerGear and found then
        headerGear.Container.Visible = found.Gear == true
        setGearIconColor(headerGear, Theme.Text)
    end

    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
        local selected = nav.Name == name
        local terminologyLabel = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
        if terminologyLabel then
            -- Match the tab's current hover color while keeping labels independent.
            local hoveringThisTab = nav.Hovering == true
            tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, {
                -- Text uses a muted perceived-match tint because opaque glyphs look
                -- brighter than translucent icon artwork at the same RGB value.
                TextColor3 = selected and terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76)
                    or (hoveringThisTab and terminologyTint(Theme.IconHover, 0.86) or Theme.IconIdle),
            })
        end
        -- The mockup has no selected tile or accent bar; only the active icon brightens.
        tween(nav.Button, 0.14, {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        })
        if nav.Icon then
            -- Selected stays bright; a hovered, unselected icon uses the same muted
            -- hover tint as its separate terminology label.
            tween(nav.Icon, 0.14, {
                ImageColor3 = selected and Theme.IconActive
                    or (nav.Hovering and Theme.IconHover or Theme.IconIdle),
                ImageTransparency = 0,
            })
            setFallbackEmphasis(nav.Button, selected)
        end
        if nav.Gear then
            setGearIconColor(nav.Gear, selected and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted)
        end
        -- The mockup has no bright side indicator or selected tile.
        nav.Outline.Transparency = 1
    end

    -- Lumen owns the real page/control frames. The page frame itself has been mounted
    -- over Syllinse's original pair of panels, so use Lumen's native Open/Close behavior.
    local lumenPage = lumenPages[name]
    if lumenPage and type(lumenPage.Open) == "function" then
        pcall(function() lumenPage:Open() end)
    end
    for tabName, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
        if page.Frame then
            page.Frame.Visible = (tabName == name)
        end
    end
    local hosts = lumenHosts[name]
    if hosts and hosts.Scroll then
        hosts.Scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    end
end

for itemIndex, item in ipairs(navItems) do
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = item.Name .. "Button"
    -- Keep the original compact navigation button geometry on all devices.
    local navTapSize = 46
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(navTapSize, navTapSize)
    button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, item.Y)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Text = ""
    button.ZIndex = 2
    button.Parent = sidebar
    addCorner(button, 13)

    local outline = addStroke(button, Theme.Stroke, 1, 1)
    -- Hover animation is deliberately limited to sidebar tab buttons (not Lumen controls).
    local hoverScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    hoverScale.Name = "TabHoverScale"
    hoverScale.Scale = 1
    hoverScale.Parent = button

    local icon
    local gearIcon
    if item.IconId then
        -- Uniform 64x64 image box for every category. Roblox texture padding is often significant;
        -- a larger box makes the visible glyph match the mockup. Fallback drawing stays 38x38.
        icon = makeImageIcon(
            item.Name .. "Icon", button, item.IconId, item.IconSize or 64,
            UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), 3, nil, 38
        )
        icon.ImageColor3 = Theme.Muted
    elseif item.Gear then
        gearIcon = makeGearIcon(
            button, 34, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0), Theme.Muted, 3, Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        )
    end

    local terminologyLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    terminologyLabel.Name = "FunctionTerminology"
    terminologyLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    terminologyLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    terminologyLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    -- This label is a sibling of the tab button, not a child of it. It therefore
    -- cannot enlarge the button's hitbox or inherit the icon's hover-scale animation.
    -- Position it just below the icon's original-size image box in the sidebar layer.
    -- The Combat icon artwork has extra visual padding, so tuck its label slightly closer.
    local terminologyLabelY = item.Y + navTapSize / 2 + (item.IconSize or 64) / 2
        + ((item.Name == "Configs" or item.Name == "Visuals") and -4 or (item.Name == "Combat" and -1 or 2))
    -- Keep Configs and Visuals terminology labels using the same vertical adjustment.
    -- Snap labels to whole-pixel coordinates for crisp text rendering.
    local terminologyLabelOffsetY = (item.Name == "Configs" or item.Name == "Visuals") and 0 or 4
    local terminologyLabelPositionY = math.floor(terminologyLabelY + terminologyLabelOffsetY + 0.5)
    terminologyLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, terminologyLabelPositionY)
    -- Match the odd-width sidebar so the centered label's left edge lands on a whole pixel.
    terminologyLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(77, 18)
    terminologyLabel.FontFace = FONT_SEMIBOLD
    terminologyLabel.Text = item.Name
    -- A slightly larger native-size glyph is easier to read than shrinking 13px text.
    -- TextScaled remains off so Roblox does not auto-fit and resample the lettering.
    terminologyLabel.TextScaled = false
    terminologyLabel.TextSize = 14
    terminologyLabel.RichText = false
    terminologyLabel.AutoLocalize = false
    terminologyLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.IconIdle
    terminologyLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    terminologyLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    terminologyLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    terminologyLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    terminologyLabel.ZIndex = 5
    terminologyLabel.Visible = showMenuFunctionTerminology
    terminologyLabel.TextTransparency = showMenuFunctionTerminology and 0 or 1
    terminologyLabel.Parent = sidebar
    terminologyLabel.Active = false
    terminologyLabel.Selectable = false
    terminologyLabels[item.Name] = terminologyLabel

    -- Keep text at its native scale so small glyphs stay crisp; the label's
    -- color tween below still provides hover feedback without resampling the text.

    -- The label is a sibling of the button, so it uses a separate visual tween.
    -- Match the icon's exact scale multiplier, duration, and easing; the label stays
    -- outside the button and never changes its clickable size or hitbox.

    local buttonPointerInside = false
    local labelPointerInside = false
    local navHoverState = false
    local navReference

    local function applyNavHover(isHovered)
        if navReference then
            navReference.Hovering = isHovered
        end
        if navHoverState ~= isHovered then
            navHoverState = isHovered
            -- Keep the icon's subtle 1.04x hover animation. Text stays at native scale
            -- so its letterforms remain crisp; the label color still animates with hover.
            tween(hoverScale, 0.16, { Scale = isHovered and 1.04 or 1 })
        end

        if isHovered then
            if item.Name ~= activeName then
                tween(button, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover, BackgroundTransparency = 0.58 })
                if icon then tween(icon, 0.14, { ImageColor3 = Theme.IconHover }) end
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconHover, 0.86) })
            else
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76) })
            end
        else
            if item.Name ~= activeName then
                tween(button, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
                if icon then tween(icon, 0.14, { ImageColor3 = Theme.IconIdle }) end
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = Theme.IconIdle })
            else
                tween(terminologyLabel, 0.14, { TextColor3 = terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76) })
            end
        end
    end

    local function deferHoverExit()
        -- MouseLeave fires as the pointer crosses the gap between the icon button
        -- and its label. Wait briefly so the independent label can claim the hover
        -- state without adding any pixels to the button's clickable hitbox.
        task.delay(0.08, function()
            if not buttonPointerInside and not labelPointerInside then
                applyNavHover(false)
            end
        end)
    end

    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        buttonPointerInside = true
        applyNavHover(true)
    end)
    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        buttonPointerInside = false
        deferHoverExit()
    end)
    terminologyLabel.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if showMenuFunctionTerminology and terminologyLabel.Visible then
            labelPointerInside = true
            applyNavHover(true)
        end
    end)
    terminologyLabel.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        labelPointerInside = false
        deferHoverExit()
    end)
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        setActiveTab(item.Name)
        -- Keep a feature query active while browsing its matches across categories.
        -- Category-prefix navigation (such as "c") clears when the user manually switches away.
        if searchBox and currentSearchMode ~= "controls" then
            searchBox.Text = ""
        end
    end)

    navReference = {
        Name = item.Name,
        Button = button,
        Icon = icon,
        Gear = gearIcon,
        Outline = outline,
        BaseButtonWidth = navTapSize,
        BaseButtonHeight = navTapSize,
        BaseButtonX = 15,
        BaseY = item.Y,
        TerminologyLabelY = math.floor(terminologyLabelY + terminologyLabelOffsetY + 0.5),
        BaseIconSize = item.IconSize or 64,
        Hovering = false,
    }
    table.insert(navButtons, navReference)
end

-- Magik Society-style window morph, adapted to Syllinse's neutral charcoal theme.
-- The main window expands out of a compact, draggable Syllinse launcher pill; closing
-- it collapses the window back into that same pill while the content fades away.
baseWindowSize = root.Size

motionBody.BorderSizePixel = 0

local function syncLauncherGlow()
    if not launcher or not launcher.Parent or not launcherGlow.Parent then return end
    local position = launcher.AbsolutePosition
    local size = launcher.AbsoluteSize
    local guiPosition = gui.AbsolutePosition
    launcherGlow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
        position.X - guiPosition.X + size.X / 2,
        position.Y - guiPosition.Y + size.Y / 2
    )
end
launcher:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsolutePosition"):Connect(syncLauncherGlow)
launcher:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(syncLauncherGlow)
task.defer(syncLauncherGlow)

local windowIsOpen = false
local windowTransitionId = 0
local setWindowOpen

local function refreshLauncherVisibility()
    local shouldShow = showLauncherButton and not windowIsOpen
    launcher.Visible = shouldShow
    launcherGlow.Visible = shouldShow
    if shouldShow then
        syncLauncherGlow()
    end
end

local function launcherBounds()
    local guiPosition = gui.AbsolutePosition
    local absolutePosition = launcher.AbsolutePosition
    local size = launcher.AbsoluteSize
    local center = UDim2.fromOffset(
        absolutePosition.X - guiPosition.X + size.X / 2,
        absolutePosition.Y - guiPosition.Y + size.Y / 2
    )
    return center, size
end

setWindowOpen = function(open)
    open = open == true
    if open == windowIsOpen then return end

    windowIsOpen = open
    windowTransitionId += 1
    local thisTransition = windowTransitionId

    if open then
        -- Keep the responsive fit scale, but remove the resting motion factor during the morph.
        windowMotionFactor.Value = 1
        uiScale.Scale = responsiveScaleValue

        local center
        local collapsedSize

        if showLauncherButton then
            local buttonSize
            center, buttonSize = launcherBounds()
            local effectiveScale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.01)
            collapsedSize = UDim2.fromOffset(
                buttonSize.X / effectiveScale,
                buttonSize.Y / effectiveScale
            )
        else
            center = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            collapsedSize = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1)
        end

        launcher.Visible = false
        launcherGlow.Visible = false
        root.Position = center
        root.Size = collapsedSize
        root.BackgroundColor3 = CLOSED_SURFACE
        root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        root.ClipsDescendants = true
        motionBody.GroupTransparency = showLauncherButton and 1 or 0
        shellStroke.Transparency = showLauncherButton and 1 or 0.12
        root.Visible = true

        -- The reference animation morphs position + size (not UIScale) with Quart InOut.
        local grow = TweenService:Create(
            root,
            TweenInfo.new(0.85, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
            {
                Position = center,
                Size = baseWindowSize,
                BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root,
            }
        )
        grow:Play()

        task.delay(0.25, function()
            if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or not windowIsOpen or not root.Parent then
                return
            end
            tween(motionBody, 0.4, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
            tween(shellStroke, 0.4, { Transparency = 0.12 })
        end)
    else
        -- Keep MacLib's hidden host enabled so its native popup and overlay descendants remain available.
        if Lumen and Lumen.OpenPopup and type(Lumen.OpenPopup.Close) == "function" then
            pcall(function() Lumen.OpenPopup.Close() end)
        end
        if Lumen and Lumen._Instance then Lumen._Instance.Enabled = true end
        if Lumen and Lumen._Overlay then Lumen._Overlay.Enabled = true end

        if not showLauncherButton then
            launcher.Visible = false
            launcherGlow.Visible = false

            local absoluteCenter = root.AbsolutePosition + root.AbsoluteSize / 2
            local guiPosition = gui.AbsolutePosition
            local center = UDim2.fromOffset(
                absoluteCenter.X - guiPosition.X,
                absoluteCenter.Y - guiPosition.Y
            )

            tween(motionBody, 0.12, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
            tween(shellStroke, 0.12, { Transparency = 1 })
            local shrinkAway = TweenService:Create(
                root,
                TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                {
                    Position = center,
                    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                }
            )
            shrinkAway:Play()
            shrinkAway.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
                if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
                if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or windowIsOpen then return end
                root.Visible = false
                root.Size = baseWindowSize
                root.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
                root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                shellStroke.Transparency = 0.12
                motionBody.GroupTransparency = 1
                windowMotionFactor.Value = 0.90
                refreshLauncherVisibility()
            end)
            return
        end

        local absoluteCenter = root.AbsolutePosition + root.AbsoluteSize / 2
        local guiPosition = gui.AbsolutePosition
        local centerX = absoluteCenter.X - guiPosition.X
        local centerY = absoluteCenter.Y - guiPosition.Y
        local center = UDim2.fromOffset(centerX, centerY)
        local effectiveScale = math.max(uiScale.Scale, 0.01)
        local buttonSize = launcher.AbsoluteSize
        local collapsedSize = UDim2.fromOffset(
            buttonSize.X / effectiveScale,
            buttonSize.Y / effectiveScale
        )

        -- Like the reference launcher, the pill follows the main window if it was dragged.
        launcher.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        launcher.Position = center
        tween(motionBody, 0.16, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
        tween(shellStroke, 0.16, { Transparency = 1 })

        local shrink = TweenService:Create(
            root,
            TweenInfo.new(0.82, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
            {
                Position = center,
                Size = collapsedSize,
                BackgroundColor3 = CLOSED_SURFACE,
            }
        )
        shrink:Play()
        shrink.Completed:Connect(function(playbackState)
            if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
            if thisTransition ~= windowTransitionId or windowIsOpen then return end

            root.Visible = false
            root.Size = baseWindowSize
            root.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Root
            root.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            shellStroke.Transparency = 0.12
            motionBody.GroupTransparency = 1
            windowMotionFactor.Value = 0.90
            refreshLauncherVisibility()
        end)
    end
end

launcher.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    tween(launcher, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover })
    tween(launcherStroke, 0.14, { Color = Theme.Dim, Transparency = 0 })
    tween(launcherGlow, 0.16, { ImageTransparency = 0.66, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(LAUNCHER_SIZE.X + 54, 98) })
end)
launcher.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    tween(launcher, 0.18, { BackgroundColor3 = CLOSED_SURFACE })
    tween(launcherStroke, 0.18, { Color = Theme.Stroke, Transparency = 0.08 })
    tween(launcherGlow, 0.22, { ImageTransparency = 0.82, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(LAUNCHER_SIZE.X + 40, 90) })
end)

-- The launcher is draggable like the reference pill; a click opens it, a drag only repositions it.
local draggingLauncher = false
local launcherMoved = false
local launcherGrab = Vector2.zero
local launcherDragStart = Vector2.zero
launcher.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        -- InputObject.Position is a Vector3 in Roblox; convert screen X/Y to Vector2
        -- before comparing it with GuiObject.AbsolutePosition (a Vector2).
        local inputPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        draggingLauncher = true
        launcherMoved = false
        launcherGrab = inputPosition - launcher.AbsolutePosition
        launcherDragStart = inputPosition
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if not draggingLauncher then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end

    local inputPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
    if (inputPosition - launcherDragStart).Magnitude > 3 then
        launcherMoved = true
    end
    if not launcherMoved then return end

    local point = inputPosition - launcherGrab - gui.AbsolutePosition
    launcher.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    launcher.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
        math.clamp(point.X, 0, math.max(gui.AbsoluteSize.X - launcher.AbsoluteSize.X, 0)),
        math.clamp(point.Y, 0, math.max(gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - launcher.AbsoluteSize.Y, 0))
    )
    syncLauncherGlow()
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if not draggingLauncher then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        return
    end

    draggingLauncher = false
    if not launcherMoved then
        setWindowOpen(true)
    end
end)

closeButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    tween(closeButton, 0.14, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Text,
    })
    tween(closeStroke, 0.14, { Transparency = 0.2 })
end)
closeButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    tween(closeButton, 0.18, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Muted,
    })
    tween(closeStroke, 0.18, { Transparency = 1 })
end)
closeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
    setWindowOpen(false)
end)

-- Search has no dropdown. After Lumen columns are mounted below, typed prefixes navigate
-- to the closest tab name, while other queries filter real controls inside the content columns.

-- Lumen's native section cards are the content panels; do not create the older pair
-- of Syllinse backdrop panels underneath them (that created duplicate, mismatched surfaces).
local PANEL_TOP = 73
local FOOTER_HEIGHT = 34
local PANEL_HEIGHT = 568 - FOOTER_HEIGHT

    "ProjectFooterBrand", root, "Syllinse Project",
