-- Reflow the chrome and content for narrow portrait phones and short landscape screens.
-- Touch layouts use nearly-native pixel sizes; only the columns stack when the content area is narrow.
layoutResponsiveShell = function()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
    local canvasW = math.max(320, root.Size.X.Offset)
    local canvasH = math.max(320, root.Size.Y.Offset)
    local compact = showLauncherButton and canvasW < 620
    compactTouchLayout = compact
    local railWidth = compact and 58 or 77
    PANEL_TOP = compact and 108 or 73

    sidebar.Size = UDim2.new(0, railWidth, 1, 0)
    sideDivider.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth - 1, 0)
    header.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth, 0)
    header.Size = UDim2.new(1, -railWidth, 0, PANEL_TOP)

    if compact then
        searchFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        searchFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 58)
        searchFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38)
        dragHandle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 54)
        headerTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 34)
        avatar.Visible = false
        logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 54)
        logo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 1)
        local logoFallback = sidebar:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
        if logoFallback then
            logoFallback.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44)
            logoFallback.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7)
        end
    else
        searchFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        searchFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0, 13)
        searchFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(canvasW - railWidth - 420, 190, 242), 46)
        avatar.Visible = canvasW >= 850
        avatar.Position = UDim2.new(1, -364, 0, 15)
        dragHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(180, math.min(470, canvasW - railWidth - 360)), 1, 0)
        headerTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 34)
        logo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(88, 96)
        logo.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-5, -7)
        local logoFallback = sidebar:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback")
        if logoFallback then
            logoFallback.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 60)
            logoFallback.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10)
        end
    end

    -- Keep the six icon targets reachable even in phone landscape.
    local navCount = #navButtons
    if navCount > 0 then
        if canvasH >= 600 and not compact then
            for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
                nav.Button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46)
                nav.Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, nav.BaseY or 122)
            end
        else
            local buttonSize = compact and 42 or 40
            local top = math.min(compact and 78 or 74, math.max(48, canvasH * 0.20))
            local bottom = 12
            local step = navCount > 1 and math.max(buttonSize + 2, (canvasH - top - bottom - buttonSize) / (navCount - 1)) or 0
            for i, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
                nav.Button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(buttonSize, buttonSize)
                nav.Button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((railWidth - buttonSize) / 2, top + (i - 1) * step)
            end
        end
    end

    FOOTER_HEIGHT = compact and 30 or 34
    footerBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerLeftText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -148, 1, 0)
    footerLeftText.TextSize = compact and 12 or 14
    footerBrand.Size = UDim2.new(0, 132, 0, FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBrand.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -FOOTER_HEIGHT)
    footerBrand.TextSize = compact and 12 or 15
    PANEL_HEIGHT = math.max(110, canvasH - PANEL_TOP - (footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0))

    for _, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
        local frame = page and page.Frame
        if frame then
            frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(railWidth + 7, PANEL_TOP)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(railWidth + 14), 1, -(PANEL_TOP + (footerBar.Visible and FOOTER_HEIGHT or 0)))
            frame.ScrollBarThickness = compact and 10 or 3
            frame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Dim
            frame.ScrollBarImageTransparency = compact and 0.15 or 0.35
            if page.RefreshResponsiveLayout then page:RefreshResponsiveLayout() end
        end
    end
    if baseWindowSize then baseWindowSize = root.Size end
end

-- Drag window from the left side of the top bar.
local dragging = false
local dragInput
local dragStart
local startPosition

dragHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPosition = root.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

dragHandle.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragInput = input
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and input == dragInput then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        root.Position = UDim2.new(
            startPosition.X.Scale,
            startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
            startPosition.Y.Scale,
            startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        )
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = false
    end
end)


-- ============================================================================
-- MACLIB NATIVE CONTROL BRIDGE
-- MacLib supplies every settings control; the Syllinse shell, navigation, search,
-- notification renderer, page layout and grayscale visual language remain native.
-- No remote Lumen source is fetched or executed.
-- ============================================================================

local function clearPreviousSyllinseControlLayers()
    local containers = { playerGui }
    local okCore, coreGui = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    if okCore and coreGui then table.insert(containers, coreGui) end
    local okHui, hui = pcall(function() return type(gethui) == "function" and gethui() or nil end)
    if okHui and hui then table.insert(containers, hui) end
    for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and (
                child:GetAttribute("SyllinseLumenBackend") == true
                or child:GetAttribute("SyllinseLumenOverlay") == true
                or child:GetAttribute("SyllinseMacLibBackend") == true
            ) then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
end
clearPreviousSyllinseControlLayers()

MacLib:SetFolder("Syllinse")
local MacLibWindow = MacLib:Window({
    Title = "Syllinse Controls",
    Subtitle = "Native MacLib controls",
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(868, 650),
    DragStyle = 1,
    DisabledWindowControls = { "Exit", "Minimize" },
    ShowUserInfo = false,
    AcrylicBlur = false,
    Keybind = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown, -- Syllinse owns the real shell toggle key.
    ToggleBtnVisible = false,
})
assert(MacLibWindow and MacLibWindow._Instance and MacLibWindow._Base, "MacLib did not create its native host")
MacLibWindow._Instance.Name = "SyllinseMacLibBackend"
MacLibWindow._Instance:SetAttribute("SyllinseMacLibBackend", true)
MacLibWindow._Instance.IgnoreGuiInset = true
MacLibWindow._Instance.DisplayOrder = (gui.DisplayOrder or 30) + 1
MacLibWindow._Instance.Enabled = true
MacLibWindow.PopupParent = motionBody
MacLibWindow._Base.Visible = false -- only the MacLib control content is reparented into Syllinse.
MacLibWindow:SetToggleBtnVisible(false)
MacLibWindow:SetHideBtnVisible(false)
local MacLibTabGroup = MacLibWindow:TabGroup()

Lumen = {
    _Instance = MacLibWindow._Instance,
    _Overlay = nil,
    Theme = {
        Background = Theme.Root,
        Surface = Theme.Surface,
        SurfaceAlt = Theme.SurfaceHover,
        Border = Theme.Stroke,
        SectionBorder = Theme.Stroke,
        Text = Theme.PanelText,
        TextDim = Theme.PanelIcon,
        Accent = Theme.Accent,
        AccentDark = Theme.Muted,
    },
    Flags = {},
    Sections = {},
    Searchable = {},
    Controls = {},
    NotifyPosition = "Top Right",
    NotifyToggles = false,
    MenuKey = nil,
    OpenPopup = { Close = function()
        for _, control in ipairs(Lumen.Controls or {}) do
            local native = control and control._native
            if native and type(native.ClosePopup) == "function" then
                pcall(function() native:ClosePopup() end)
            end
        end
    end },
}

local function safeConfigName(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
    name = name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    if name == "" or name:find("..", 1, true) or name:find("/", 1, true) or name:find("\\", 1, true) then
        return nil
    end
    return name
end

function Lumen.SetConfigFolder(folder)
    if type(folder) == "string" and folder ~= "" then
        MacLib:SetFolder(folder)
    end
end
function Lumen.ListConfigs()
    local result = MacLib:RefreshConfigList()
    return type(result) == "table" and result or {}
end
function Lumen.SaveConfig(name)
    name = safeConfigName(name)
    if not name then return false, "Invalid config name." end
    local ok, result, detail = pcall(function()
        local a, b = MacLib:SaveConfig(name)
        return a, b
    end)
    if not ok then return false, result end
    return result == true, detail or result
end
function Lumen.LoadConfig(name)
    name = safeConfigName(name)
    if not name then return false, "Invalid config name." end
    local ok, result, detail = pcall(function()
        local a, b = MacLib:LoadConfig(name)
        return a, b
    end)
    if not ok then return false, result end
    return result == true, detail or result
end
function Lumen.DeleteConfig(name)
    name = safeConfigName(name)
    if not name then return false, "Invalid config name." end
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(delfile) ~= "function" then
        return false, "File-delete APIs are unavailable."
    end
    local path = MacLib.Folder .. "/settings/" .. name .. ".json"
    local ok, exists = pcall(isfile, path)
    if not ok or not exists then return false, "Config file not found." end
    local deleted, err = pcall(delfile, path)
    return deleted, err
end
function Lumen.SetWatermark(_title, _state)
    -- The Syllinse shell owns its branding; MacLib's hidden watermark is intentionally unused.
end
function Lumen.SetKeybindList(_state)
    -- A separate keybind-list overlay is not part of the current Syllinse design.
end

local function normalizeMultiSelection(options, value)
    if type(value) ~= "table" then return {} end
    local output = {}
    for _, option in ipairs(options or {}) do
        if value[option] == true or table.find(value, option) then
            table.insert(output, option)
        end
    end
    if #output == 0 then
        for _, option in ipairs(value) do
            if type(option) == "string" and not table.find(output, option) then table.insert(output, option) end
        end
    end
    return output
end

local function makeSectionTheme(sectionFrame)
    if not sectionFrame or not sectionFrame.Parent then return end
    sectionFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Lumen.Theme.Surface or Theme.Surface
    sectionFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    for _, d in ipairs(sectionFrame:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
            d.Color = Lumen.Theme.SectionBorder or Theme.Stroke
            d.Transparency = 0.35
        elseif d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
            d.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
            d.AutoLocalize = false
            d.TextScaled = false
            if d:IsA("TextBox") then
                d.TextColor3 = Lumen.Theme.Text or Theme.PanelText
                d.PlaceholderColor3 = Theme.PanelSecondary
                d.BackgroundColor3 = Lumen.Theme.SurfaceAlt or Theme.SurfaceHover
            elseif d.Name == "HeaderText" or d.Name == "ParagraphHeader" then
                d.FontFace = FONT_MEDIUM
                d.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelTitle
                d.TextSize = math.max(d.TextSize, 15)
            else
                d.TextColor3 = Lumen.Theme.Text or Theme.PanelText
                d.TextSize = math.clamp(d.TextSize, 14, 16)
            end
        elseif d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then
            local lowerName = string.lower(d.Name or "")
            if lowerName:find("slider", 1, true) then
                d.ImageColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
            elseif lowerName ~= "newcolor" and lowerName ~= "oldcolor" then
                d.ImageColor3 = Theme.PanelIcon
            end
        end
    end
end

function Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections) do
        makeSectionTheme(section.Frame)
    end
end

local function registerFlag(flag, control, initialValue)
    if type(flag) ~= "string" or flag == "" then return end
    Lumen.Flags[flag] = Lumen.Flags[flag] or {}
    Lumen.Flags[flag].Value = initialValue
    Lumen.Flags[flag].Set = control.Set
    Lumen.Flags[flag].Control = control
end

local function rememberControl(control, section, frame, labelText)
    control.Frame = frame
    control.Flag = control.Flag or control.Settings and control.Settings.Flag
    control.Section = section
    table.insert(Lumen.Controls, control)
    if frame then
        frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseControl", true)
        makeSectionTheme(section.Frame)
        table.insert(Lumen.Searchable, {
            Frame = frame,
            Text = tostring(labelText or control.Settings and (control.Settings.Name or control.Settings.Title or control.Settings.Text) or ""),
            Section = section,
        })
    end
    registerFlag(control.Flag, control, control.Value)
    return control
end

local function newestGuiChild(sectionFrame, before)
    local candidate, bestOrder
    for _, child in ipairs(sectionFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiObject") and not before[child] then
            local order = tonumber(child.LayoutOrder) or 0
            if not candidate or order >= bestOrder then
                candidate, bestOrder = child, order
            end
        end
    end
    return candidate
end

local function createControl(section, kind, options)
    local opts = options or {}
    local flag = opts.Flag
    local rawSection = section._raw
    local before = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(rawSection._frame:GetChildren()) do before[child] = true end
    local suppressCallback = false
    local control = { Settings = opts, Flag = flag, Class = kind, Value = nil, State = false }

    local function setValue(value)
        control.Value = value
        if kind == "Toggle" then control.State = value == true end
        if kind == "Keybind" then control.Key = value end
        if kind == "Colorpicker" then control.Color = value end
        if type(flag) == "string" and flag ~= "" then
            Lumen.Flags[flag] = Lumen.Flags[flag] or {}
            Lumen.Flags[flag].Value = value
            Lumen.Flags[flag].Set = control.Set
            Lumen.Flags[flag].Control = control
        end
    end

    local nativeSettings
    local native
    if kind == "Toggle" then
        local default = opts.State == true
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Name or section.Name,
            Default = default,
            Callback = function(value)
                setValue(value == true)
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(value == true) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Toggle(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local value, silent
            if a == control then value, silent = b, c else value, silent = a, b end
            value = value == true
            if control.State == value then return value end
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:UpdateState(value)
            setValue(value)
            suppressCallback = false
            return value
        end
        control.GetState = function() return control.State end
        setValue(default)
    elseif kind == "Slider" then
        local minimum = tonumber(opts.Min or opts.Minimum) or 0
        local maximum = tonumber(opts.Max or opts.Maximum) or 100
        local step = math.abs(tonumber(opts.Increment) or 1)
        if step <= 0 then step = 1 end
        local initial = tonumber(opts.Value or opts.Default) or minimum
        local function snap(value)
            value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or minimum, minimum, maximum)
            return math.clamp(minimum + math.floor(((value - minimum) / step) + 0.5) * step, minimum, maximum)
        end
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Name or "Slider",
            Minimum = minimum,
            Maximum = maximum,
            Default = snap(initial),
            Precision = tonumber(opts.Precision) or (step % 1 == 0 and 0 or 2),
            Suffix = opts.Suffix or "",
            Prefix = opts.Prefix or "",
            Callback = function(value)
                local snapped = snap(value)
                setValue(snapped)
                if math.abs((tonumber(value) or snapped) - snapped) > 0.0001 and native then
                    suppressCallback = true
                    native:UpdateValue(snapped, true)
                    suppressCallback = false
                    setValue(snapped)
                end
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(snapped) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Slider(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local value, silent
            if a == control then value, silent = b, c else value, silent = a, b end
            value = snap(value)
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:UpdateValue(value, true)
            setValue(value)
            suppressCallback = false
            return value
        end
        control.GetValue = function() return control.Value end
        control.UpdateValue = control.Set
        setValue(snap(initial))
    elseif kind == "Dropdown" then
        local optionsList = type(opts.Options) == "table" and table.clone(opts.Options) or {}
        local multi = opts.Multi == true
        local oldValue = opts.Value
        local default = oldValue
        if multi then
            default = normalizeMultiSelection(optionsList, oldValue)
        elseif type(oldValue) == "string" then
            default = table.find(optionsList, oldValue) -- MacLib initializes single selection by option index.
        elseif type(oldValue) == "number" then
            default = oldValue
        else
            default = nil
        end
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Name or "Dropdown",
            Options = optionsList,
            Default = default,
            Multi = multi,
            Required = opts.Required == true,
            Search = opts.Search == true,
            Callback = function(value)
                local normalized = multi and normalizeMultiSelection(optionsList, value) or value
                setValue(normalized)
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(normalized) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Dropdown(nativeSettings, flag)
        local initialValue = multi and normalizeMultiSelection(optionsList, oldValue) or oldValue
        if initialValue == nil or initialValue == "" then initialValue = multi and {} or "" end
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local value, silent
            if a == control then value, silent = b, c else value, silent = a, b end
            if multi then value = normalizeMultiSelection(optionsList, value) end
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:UpdateSelection(value)
            setValue(value)
            suppressCallback = false
            return value
        end
        control.UpdateOptions = function(a, b)
            local values = a == control and b or a
            values = type(values) == "table" and table.clone(values) or {}
            local previous = control.Value
            native:ClearOptions()
            native:InsertOptions(values)
            optionsList = values
            nativeSettings.Options = values
            opts.Options = values
            if multi then
                local retain = normalizeMultiSelection(values, previous)
                control.Set(retain, true)
            elseif type(previous) == "string" and table.find(values, previous) then
                control.Set(previous, true)
            elseif #values > 0 then
                control.Set(values[1], true)
            else
                control.Value = ""
                if type(flag) == "string" and Lumen.Flags[flag] then Lumen.Flags[flag].Value = "" end
            end
        end
        control.ClearOptions = function() native:ClearOptions(); optionsList = {}; control.Options = optionsList; control.Value = multi and {} or "" end
        control.Options = optionsList
        control.GetValue = function() return control.Value end
        setValue(initialValue)
        -- MacLib's dropdown options popup needs to remain above the Syllinse shell.
        if Lumen._Instance then Lumen._Instance.DisplayOrder = (gui.DisplayOrder or 30) + 1 end
    elseif kind == "Input" then
        local initial = tostring(opts.Value or opts.Default or "")
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Name or "Input",
            Default = initial,
            Placeholder = opts.Placeholder or "",
            AcceptedCharacters = opts.AcceptedCharacters or "All",
            CharacterLimit = opts.CharacterLimit,
            Callback = function(value)
                value = tostring(value or "")
                setValue(value)
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(value) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Input(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local value, silent
            if a == control then value, silent = b, c else value, silent = a, b end
            value = tostring(value or "")
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:UpdateText(value)
            setValue(value)
            suppressCallback = false
            return value
        end
        control.GetText = function() return control.Value end
        control.UpdateText = control.Set
        setValue(initial)
    elseif kind == "Keybind" then
        local requestedKey = opts.Key or opts.Default or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
        local mode = opts.Type or "Toggle"
        local active = false
        local function handleKeyDown(key)
            if mode == "Hold" then return end
            active = not active
            setValue(requestedKey)
            if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(active) end
        end
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Title or opts.Name or section.Name,
            Default = requestedKey,
            Blacklist = opts.Blacklist,
            Callback = function(key)
                requestedKey = key
                setValue(key)
                if mode ~= "Hold" then handleKeyDown(key) end
            end,
            onBindHeld = function(held, key)
                requestedKey = key or requestedKey
                setValue(requestedKey)
                if mode == "Hold" and not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then
                    opts.Callback(held == true)
                end
            end,
            onBinded = function(key)
                requestedKey = key
                setValue(key)
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.onBinded) == "function" then opts.onBinded(key) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Keybind(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Key = requestedKey
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local key, silent
            if a == control then key, silent = b, c else key, silent = a, b end
            requestedKey = key or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:Bind(requestedKey)
            setValue(requestedKey)
            suppressCallback = false
            if not silent and type(opts.onBinded) == "function" then opts.onBinded(requestedKey) end
            return requestedKey
        end
        control.GetBind = function() return requestedKey end
        setValue(requestedKey)
    elseif kind == "Colorpicker" then
        local defaultColor = typeof(opts.Color) == "Color3" and opts.Color or (typeof(opts.Default) == "Color3" and opts.Default) or Theme.Text
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Title or opts.Name or section.Name,
            Default = defaultColor,
            Alpha = opts.Alpha,
            Callback = function(color, alpha)
                setValue(color)
                control.Color = color
                control.Alpha = alpha
                if not suppressCallback and type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback(color, alpha) end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Colorpicker(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Set = function(a, b, c)
            local color, silent
            if a == control then color, silent = b, c else color, silent = a, b end
            if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return control.Color end
            suppressCallback = silent == true
            native:SetColor(color)
            setValue(color)
            control.Color = color
            suppressCallback = false
            return color
        end
        control.SetColor = control.Set
        control.GetColor = function() return control.Color end
        setValue(defaultColor)
        control.Color = defaultColor
    elseif kind == "Button" then
        nativeSettings = {
            Name = opts.Name or "Button",
            Description = opts.Description,
            DoubleClick = opts.DoubleClick,
            Callback = function()
                if type(opts.Callback) == "function" then opts.Callback() end
            end,
        }
        native = rawSection:Button(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.Set = function() return false end
    elseif kind == "Paragraph" then
        nativeSettings = {
            Header = opts.Title or opts.Header or "",
            Body = opts.Body or "",
        }
        native = rawSection:Paragraph(nativeSettings, flag)
        control.UpdateHeader = function(a, b) native:UpdateHeader(a == control and b or a) end
        control.UpdateBody = function(a, b) native:UpdateBody(a == control and b or a) end
        control.SetVisibility = function(a, b) native:SetVisibility(a == control and b or a) end
        control.Set = function(value) control.Value = value end
    else
        error("Unsupported MacLib control: " .. tostring(kind))
    end

    control._native = native
    control.Settings = opts
    control.Flag = flag
    local frame = newestGuiChild(rawSection._frame, before)
    rememberControl(control, section, frame, opts.Name or opts.Title or opts.Text or opts.Header)
    if kind == "Keybind" and frame then
        -- Keep Syllinse's key capture guard aware of MacLib's native TextBox binder.
        local keyBox = frame:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextBox", true)
        if keyBox then
            keyBox.Name = "KeyButton"
        end
    end
    return control
end

local function makeLabelAdapter(section, settings)
    settings = settings or {}
    local before = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(section._raw._frame:GetChildren()) do before[child] = true end
    local native = section._raw:Label({ Text = settings.Text or settings.Name or "" }, settings.Flag)
    local frame = newestGuiChild(section._raw._frame, before)
    local adapter = {
        Settings = settings,
        Text = tostring(settings.Text or settings.Name or ""),
        Flag = settings.Flag,
        Frame = frame,
        _native = native,
        _section = section,
        RightContent = nil,
    }
    function adapter:UpdateName(value)
        adapter.Text = tostring(value or "")
        native:UpdateName(adapter.Text)
    end
    function adapter:SetVisibility(state)
        state = state == true
        if frame then
            frame.Visible = state
            frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseHiddenLabel", not state)
        end
        native:SetVisibility(state)
        local found = false
        for index = #Lumen.Searchable, 1, -1 do
            if Lumen.Searchable[index].Frame == frame then
                if state then
                    found = true
                else
                    table.remove(Lumen.Searchable, index)
                end
            end
        end
        if state and frame and not found then
            table.insert(Lumen.Searchable, { Frame = frame, Text = adapter.Text, Section = section })
        end
    end
    function adapter:Toggle(options)
        local merged = table.clone(options or {})
        merged.Name = merged.Name or adapter.Text
        local control = createControl(section, "Toggle", merged)
        adapter:SetVisibility(false)
        return control
    end
    function adapter:Keybind(options)
        local merged = table.clone(options or {})
        merged.Title = merged.Title or merged.Name or (adapter.Text .. " key")
        local control = createControl(section, "Keybind", merged)
        adapter:SetVisibility(false)
        return control
    end
    function adapter:Colorpicker(options)
        local merged = table.clone(options or {})
        merged.Title = merged.Title or merged.Name or adapter.Text
        local control = createControl(section, "Colorpicker", merged)
        adapter:SetVisibility(false)
        return control
    end
    if frame and settings.Color then
        local label = frame:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
        if label and typeof(settings.Color) == "Color3" then label.TextColor3 = settings.Color end
    end
    if frame then
        frame:SetAttribute("SyllinseControl", true)
        table.insert(Lumen.Searchable, { Frame = frame, Text = adapter.Text, Section = section })
    end
    return adapter
end

function Lumen:Window(_settings)
    local window = { Canvas = MacLibWindow._Base }
    function window:Page(settings)
        settings = settings or {}
        local pageName = settings.Name or "Page"
        local rawPage = MacLibTabGroup:Tab({ Name = pageName, ContentParent = motionBody })
        local pageFrame = rawPage.Frame
        pageFrame.Name = "SyllinseMacLibPage_" .. pageName
        pageFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        pageFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
        pageFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        pageFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        pageFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pageFrame.Visible = false
        pageFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
        pageFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        pageFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
        pageFrame.ScrollBarThickness = showLauncherButton and 10 or 3
        pageFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Dim
        pageFrame.ScrollBarImageTransparency = showLauncherButton and 0.15 or 0.35
        local page = { Name = pageName, Frame = pageFrame, _raw = rawPage, Left = rawPage.Left, Right = rawPage.Right }
        function page:Open()
            rawPage:Select()
            pageFrame.Parent = motionBody
            pageFrame.Visible = true
            if type(rawPage.RefreshResponsiveLayout) == "function" then rawPage:RefreshResponsiveLayout() end
        end
        function page:RefreshResponsiveLayout()
            if type(rawPage.RefreshResponsiveLayout) == "function" then rawPage:RefreshResponsiveLayout() end
        end
        function page:Section(sectionSettings)
            sectionSettings = sectionSettings or {}
            local rawSection = rawPage:Section({ Name = sectionSettings.Name or "Section", Side = sectionSettings.Side or "Left" })
            local section = {
                Name = sectionSettings.Name or "Section",
                Frame = rawSection._frame,
                PageFrame = pageFrame,
                _raw = rawSection,
                _page = page,
            }
            table.insert(Lumen.Sections, section)
            rawSection:Header({ Name = section.Name })
            makeSectionTheme(section.Frame)
            function section:Label(opts) return makeLabelAdapter(section, opts) end
            function section:Toggle(opts) return createControl(section, "Toggle", opts) end
            function section:Slider(opts) return createControl(section, "Slider", opts) end
            function section:Dropdown(opts) return createControl(section, "Dropdown", opts) end
            function section:Input(opts) return createControl(section, "Input", opts) end
            function section:Keybind(opts) return createControl(section, "Keybind", opts) end
            function section:Colorpicker(opts) return createControl(section, "Colorpicker", opts) end
            function section:Button(opts) return createControl(section, "Button", opts) end
            function section:Paragraph(opts) return createControl(section, "Paragraph", opts) end
            function section:Header(opts)
                local before = {}
                for _, child in ipairs(rawSection._frame:GetChildren()) do before[child] = true end
                local nativeHeader = rawSection:Header({ Name = (opts and (opts.Name or opts.Text)) or "" })
                local frame = newestGuiChild(rawSection._frame, before)
                local result = { _native = nativeHeader, Frame = frame }
                return result
            end
            return section
        end
        return page
    end
    return window
end

function Lumen.SetFlag(flag, value)
    if type(flag) ~= "string" then return false end
    local entry = Lumen.Flags[flag]
    if entry and type(entry.Set) == "function" then
        entry.Set(value, true)
        entry.Value = value
        return true
    end
    local option = MacLib:GetOption(flag)
    if not option then return false end
    if option.UpdateState then option:UpdateState(value == true)
    elseif option.UpdateValue then option:UpdateValue(value)
    elseif option.UpdateSelection then option:UpdateSelection(value)
    elseif option.UpdateText then option:UpdateText(tostring(value or ""))
    elseif option.SetColor then option:SetColor(value)
    elseif option.Bind then option:Bind(value) end
    return true
end

local LumenWindow = Lumen:Window({ Title = "Syllinse", Footer = "Syllinse UI" })
assert(LumenWindow and LumenWindow.Canvas, "MacLib compatibility host did not initialize")
LumenWindow.Canvas.Visible = false

-- Magik Society-style toast notifications, rebuilt with the current Syllinse palette.
-- The layout keeps the source UI's soft shadow, thin top highlight, optional icon,
-- close button, slide animation and duration progress bar without its purple accent.
local notificationHost
local notificationLayout
local notificationPosition = "Top Right"
local notificationOrder = 0
local activeNotificationDismissals = {}
local MAX_SYLLINSE_NOTIFICATIONS = 4
local NOTIFICATION_SHADOW_ASSET = "rbxassetid://6014261993"
local NOTIFICATION_GLOW_ASSET = "rbxassetid://8992230677"
local NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS = {
    Success = Theme.Text,
    Info = Theme.PanelTitle,
    Warning = Theme.PanelText,
    Error = Theme.Text,
}

local function getNotificationWidth()
    if showLauncherButton then
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local viewportWidth = camera and camera.ViewportSize.X or 390
        -- Keep mobile toasts compact and within the available viewport width.
        return math.floor(math.clamp(viewportWidth * 0.49, 178, 228))
    end
    return 248
end

local function getNotificationInset()
    return showLauncherButton and 10 or 16
end

local function getNotificationHost()
    if notificationHost and notificationHost.Parent then
        return notificationHost
    end

    local parent = (Lumen and Lumen._Instance and Lumen._Instance:IsA("ScreenGui")) and Lumen._Instance or gui
    local inset = getNotificationInset()
    notificationHost = Instance.new("Frame")
    notificationHost.Name = "SyllinseNotifications"
    notificationHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    notificationHost.Position = UDim2.new(1, -inset, 0, inset)
    notificationHost.Size = UDim2.new(0, getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    notificationHost.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    notificationHost.BorderSizePixel = 0
    notificationHost.ZIndex = 80
    notificationHost.Parent = parent

    notificationLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    notificationLayout.Name = "ToastLayout"
    notificationLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    notificationLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    notificationLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    notificationLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    notificationLayout.Parent = notificationHost

    return notificationHost
end

local function setNotificationPosition(position)
    if type(position) ~= "string" then return false end
    if not table.find({ "Top Left", "Top Right", "Bottom Left", "Bottom Right" }, position) then
        return false
    end

    notificationPosition = position
    local host = getNotificationHost()
    local left = position == "Top Left" or position == "Bottom Left"
    local bottom = position == "Bottom Left" or position == "Bottom Right"
    local inset = getNotificationInset()
    host.Size = UDim2.new(0, getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    host.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(left and 0 or 1, bottom and 1 or 0)
    host.Position = UDim2.new(left and 0 or 1, left and inset or -inset, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -inset or inset)
    if notificationLayout then
        notificationLayout.HorizontalAlignment = left and Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
        notificationLayout.VerticalAlignment = bottom and Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    end
    return true
end

local function syncNotificationHostToViewport()
    if not notificationHost or not notificationHost.Parent then return end
    local inset = getNotificationInset()
    local left = notificationPosition == "Top Left" or notificationPosition == "Bottom Left"
    local bottom = notificationPosition == "Bottom Left" or notificationPosition == "Bottom Right"
    notificationHost.Size = UDim2.new(0, getNotificationWidth(), 1, -(inset * 2))
    notificationHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(left and 0 or 1, bottom and 1 or 0)
    notificationHost.Position = UDim2.new(left and 0 or 1, left and inset or -inset, bottom and 1 or 0, bottom and -inset or inset)
end

local function makeToastGlow(parent)
    local bloom = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    bloom.Name = "ToastGlow"
    bloom.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    bloom.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, -8)
    bloom.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 145 or 180, showLauncherButton and 62 or 76)
    bloom.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bloom.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bloom.Image = NOTIFICATION_GLOW_ASSET
    bloom.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    bloom.ImageTransparency = 0.94
    bloom.ZIndex = 82
    bloom.Parent = parent

    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.Text),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.PanelIcon),
    })
    gradient.Rotation = 90
    gradient.Parent = bloom
    return bloom
end

local function showNotification(options)
    options = type(options) == "table" and options or {}
    local duration = math.clamp(tonumber(options.Duration) or 3.5, 0.5, 20)
    local title = tostring(options.Title or "Notification")
    local bodyText = tostring(options.Content or options.Text or options.Message or "")
    local titleColor = NOTIFICATION_TITLE_COLORS[options.Type] or Theme.PanelTitle

    notificationOrder += 1
    local host = getNotificationHost()
    local fromLeft = notificationPosition == "Top Left" or notificationPosition == "Bottom Left"
    local slideDistance = host.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and (host.AbsoluteSize.X + 18) or (getNotificationWidth() + 18)
    local enterX = fromLeft and -slideDistance or slideDistance

    local outer = Instance.new("Frame")
    outer.Name = "ToastOuter"
    outer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    outer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    outer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    outer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    outer.LayoutOrder = notificationOrder
    outer.ZIndex = 80
    outer.Parent = host

    local slide = Instance.new("Frame")
    slide.Name = "ToastSlide"
    slide.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(enterX, 0)
    slide.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    slide.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    slide.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    slide.BorderSizePixel = 0
    slide.ZIndex = 80
    slide.Parent = outer

    local shadow = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    shadow.Name = "ToastShadow"
    shadow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-14, -14)
    shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 28, 1, 28)
    shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shadow.Image = NOTIFICATION_SHADOW_ASSET
    shadow.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    shadow.ImageTransparency = 1
    shadow.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
    shadow.SliceCenter = Rect.new(49, 49, 450, 450)
    shadow.ZIndex = 79
    shadow.Parent = slide

    local toast = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
    toast.Name = "ToastCard"
    toast.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    toast.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    toast.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
    toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
    toast.GroupTransparency = 1
    toast.ZIndex = 81
    toast.Parent = slide
    addCorner(toast, 8)
    -- Fixed neutral outline: the border does not animate its color or thickness.
    -- It stays attached to the toast card for its entire visible lifetime.
    addStroke(toast, Color3.fromRGB(58, 57, 57), 0, 1)

    local edge = Instance.new("Frame")
    edge.Name = "TopHighlight"
    edge.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
    edge.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
    edge.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
    edge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
    edge.BorderSizePixel = 0
    edge.ZIndex = 83
    edge.Parent = toast

    makeToastGlow(toast)

    local inner = Instance.new("Frame")
    inner.Name = "ToastContent"
    inner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    inner.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    inner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    inner.BorderSizePixel = 0
    inner.ZIndex = 84
    inner.Parent = toast

    local contentPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    local toastSidePadding = showLauncherButton and 10 or 11
    contentPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, toastSidePadding)
    contentPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, toastSidePadding)
    contentPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, showLauncherButton and 8 or 9)
    contentPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, showLauncherButton and 13 or 15)
    contentPadding.Parent = inner

    local titleOffset = 3
    if options.Icon then
        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.Name = "ToastIcon"
        icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1)
        icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 12 or 13, showLauncherButton and 12 or 13)
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
        icon.Image = tostring(options.Icon)
        icon.ImageColor3 = titleColor
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        icon.ZIndex = 85
        icon.Parent = inner
        titleOffset = 21
    end

    local toastTitleSize = showLauncherButton and 14 or 15
    local toastTitleHeight = showLauncherButton and 18 or 19
    local closeReservedWidth = showLauncherButton and 34 or 27
    local titleLabel = makeText(
        "ToastTitle", inner, title,
        UDim2.new(1, -closeReservedWidth - titleOffset, 0, toastTitleHeight),
        UDim2.fromOffset(titleOffset, 0), toastTitleSize, titleColor, FONT_MEDIUM
    )
    titleLabel.ZIndex = 85

    local closeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeButton.Name = "ToastClose"
    closeButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    closeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, 2, 0, showLauncherButton and -5 or -3)
    closeButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(showLauncherButton and 28 or 22, showLauncherButton and 28 or 22)
    closeButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    closeButton.Text = "×"
    closeButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
    closeButton.TextSize = showLauncherButton and 19 or 17
    closeButton.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
    closeButton.AutoButtonColor = false
    closeButton.ZIndex = 85
    closeButton.Parent = inner
    closeButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        tween(closeButton, 0.15, { TextColor3 = Theme.Text })
    end)
    closeButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        tween(closeButton, 0.2, { TextColor3 = Theme.Muted })
    end)

    if bodyText ~= "" then
        local toastBodySize = showLauncherButton and 12 or 13
        local toastBodyTop = showLauncherButton and 20 or 21
        local contentLabel = makeText(
            "ToastMessage", inner, bodyText,
            UDim2.new(1, -12 - titleOffset, 0, 0),
            UDim2.fromOffset(titleOffset, toastBodyTop), toastBodySize, Theme.PanelText, FONT_REGULAR
        )
        contentLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        contentLabel.TextWrapped = true
        contentLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
        contentLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        contentLabel.ZIndex = 85
    end

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Name = "ToastProgressTrack"
    track.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, toastSidePadding, 1, -5)
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(toastSidePadding * 2), 0, 2)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Stroke
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.ZIndex = 85
    track.Parent = toast
    addCorner(track, 2)

    local progress = Instance.new("Frame")
    progress.Name = "ToastProgress"
    progress.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    progress.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderHead
    progress.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progress.ZIndex = 86
    progress.Parent = track
    addCorner(progress, 2)

    local dismissed = false
    local dismiss
    dismiss = function()
        if dismissed then return end
        dismissed = true
        for i, entry in ipairs(activeNotificationDismissals) do
            if entry == dismiss then
                table.remove(activeNotificationDismissals, i)
                break
            end
        end
        tween(slide, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(enterX, 0) })
        tween(toast, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
        tween(shadow, 0.2, { ImageTransparency = 1 })
        task.delay(0.24, function()
            if outer.Parent then outer:Destroy() end
        end)
    end

    closeButton.Activated:Connect(dismiss)
    table.insert(activeNotificationDismissals, dismiss)
    while #activeNotificationDismissals > MAX_SYLLINSE_NOTIFICATIONS do
        local oldest = table.remove(activeNotificationDismissals, 1)
        if oldest then oldest() end
    end

    task.defer(function()
        if outer.Parent then
            tween(slide, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) })
            tween(toast, 0.3, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
            tween(shadow, 0.4, { ImageTransparency = 0.62 })
            TweenService:Create(progress, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1),
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    task.delay(duration, dismiss)
    return { Dismiss = dismiss }
end

Lumen.Notify = showNotification
Lumen.NotifyToggles = false

if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
    Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(syncNotificationHostToViewport)
end
Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(syncNotificationHostToViewport)
    end
    syncNotificationHostToViewport()
end)

local function notify(title, message, kind, duration)
    if featureSettings.Notifications == false then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        showNotification({
            Title = tostring(title or "Syllinse"),
            Content = tostring(message or ""),
            Type = kind or "Info",
            Duration = duration or 2.5,
        })
    end)
    if not ok then warn("[Syllinse] Notification error: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- Per-feature notification opt-ins. The Settings-page Notifications switch is the
-- global master; these individual bells decide which feature toggles may toast.
local featureNotificationSettings = {}
featureSettings.FeatureNotifications = featureNotificationSettings
-- Only controls that also expose a keybind/settings gear get an inline bell.
-- Visual-only toggles and settings without a keybind control intentionally have no bell.
local FEATURE_NOTIFICATION_FLAGS = {
    AimbotEnabled = true,
    SilentAim = true,
    TriggerEnabled = true,
    AAEnabled = true,
    Fly = true,
}

local function attachFeatureNotificationBell(label, toggleControl, featureFlag)
    if not label or not label.RightContent or not toggleControl or not featureFlag then return end
    local rightContent = label.RightContent
    if not rightContent.Parent then return end

    -- The bell belongs only beside a real keybind/settings gear. Some toggles do not
    -- have a keybind control, and must not get an orphan notification icon.
    local settingsIcon = rightContent:FindFirstChild("Settings")
    if not settingsIcon or not settingsIcon:IsA("GuiObject") then return end

    local existingBell = rightContent:FindFirstChild("FeatureNotificationBell_" .. featureFlag)
    if existingBell then return existingBell end

    if featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == nil then
        featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = false
    end
    toggleControl.NotifyEnabled = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true

    local bell = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    bell.Name = "FeatureNotificationBell_" .. featureFlag
    bell.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
    bell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    bell.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
    bell.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bell.AutoButtonColor = false
    bell.Image = "rbxassetid://10709775704"
    bell.ImageColor3 = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
    bell.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    bell.LayoutOrder = 1
    bell.ZIndex = 8
    bell.Parent = rightContent

    -- Keep the bell immediately LEFT of the keybind/settings gear. Other inline
    -- controls (such as a color picker) stay after the bell/gear pair.
    for _, sibling in ipairs(rightContent:GetChildren()) do
        if sibling:IsA("GuiObject") and sibling ~= bell then
            if sibling == settingsIcon then
                sibling.LayoutOrder = 2
            else
                sibling.LayoutOrder = 3 + math.max(0, tonumber(sibling.LayoutOrder) or 0)
            end
        end
    end
    bell.LayoutOrder = 1

    bell:SetAttribute("FeatureFlag", featureFlag)
    bell:SetAttribute("Tooltip", "Toggle notifications for " .. tostring(label.Text or featureFlag))
    addCorner(bell, 4)

    local activeDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    activeDot.Name = "NotificationEnabledDot"
    activeDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4)
    activeDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    activeDot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 0)
    activeDot.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
    activeDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    activeDot.Visible = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
    activeDot.ZIndex = 9
    activeDot.Parent = bell
    addCorner(activeDot, 4)

    local function refreshBell()
        local enabled = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
        toggleControl.NotifyEnabled = enabled
        featureSettings.FeatureNotifications[featureFlag] = enabled
        bell.ImageColor3 = enabled and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
        activeDot.Visible = enabled
    end

    bell.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        bell.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
        bell.ImageColor3 = Theme.Text
    end)
    bell.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        bell.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        bell.ImageColor3 = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
    end)
    bell.Activated:Connect(function()
        featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = not (featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true)
        refreshBell()
        -- Deliberately do not toast here: clicking the bell changes preference only.
    end)

    refreshBell()
    return bell
end

local NOTIFICATION_POSITIONS = { "Top Left", "Top Right", "Bottom Left", "Bottom Right" }
local function applyNotificationPosition(position)
    if type(position) ~= "string" or not table.find(NOTIFICATION_POSITIONS, position) then
        return false
    end
    Lumen.NotifyPosition = position
    return setNotificationPosition(position)
end

setNotificationPosition(Lumen.NotifyPosition or "Top Right")

-- Normalize MacLib toggle setter behavior treats a false argument as a toggle
-- request. Normalize that edge case once per toggle so false defaults and config loads
-- remain accurate while retaining Lumen's native animation/callback/flag behavior.
local function addToggle(label, options)
    local opts = options or {}
    local requestedState = opts.State == true
    local featureFlag = type(opts.Flag) == "string" and opts.Flag or nil
    local hasFeatureNotifications = featureFlag ~= nil and FEATURE_NOTIFICATION_FLAGS[featureFlag] == true
    if hasFeatureNotifications then
        if featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == nil then
            featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] = false
        end
        opts.NotifyEnabled = featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true
    else
        -- Do not let unrelated controls emit native toggle toasts.
        opts.NotifyEnabled = false
    end
    local control = label:Toggle(opts)
    if hasFeatureNotifications then
        task.defer(function()
            attachFeatureNotificationBell(label, control, featureFlag)
        end)
    end
    local originalSet = control.Set
    local currentState = control.State == true

    control.Set = function(state, silent)
        if state == nil then state = not currentState end
        state = state == true
        if state == currentState then
            return currentState
        end
        currentState = state
        originalSet(state, silent)
        if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Value = currentState
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
        end
        if hasFeatureNotifications and not silent and featureNotificationSettings[featureFlag] == true then
            notify(opts.Name or featureFlag, currentState and "Enabled" or "Disabled",
                currentState and "Success" or "Info", opts.NotificationDuration or 2.5)
        end
        return currentState
    end

    if control.Flag then
        Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] = Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] or {}
        Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
    end
    if currentState ~= requestedState then
        control.Set(requestedState, true)
    end
    return control
end

local function attachKeybindSetting(label, options, settingName, linkedToggleControl)
    -- Lumen's Keybind callback reports the active state, but does not automatically
    -- toggle the feature's separate main switch. Link those two controls so a hotkey
    -- actually updates the same state as clicking the feature toggle.
    local keybindOptions = options or {}
    local originalCallback = keybindOptions.Callback
    if linkedToggleControl then
        keybindOptions.Callback = function(active)
            if type(originalCallback) == "function" then
                originalCallback(active)
            end
            if linkedToggleControl and type(linkedToggleControl.Set) == "function" then
                -- Use the explicit active state (not Set() with no argument), so
                -- Hold mode turns off on release and Toggle mode follows its key state.
                linkedToggleControl.Set(active)
            end
        end
    end

    local control = label:Keybind(keybindOptions)
    if settingName then
        featureSettings[settingName] = control.Key
        local originalSet = control.Set
        control.Set = function(key)
            originalSet(key)
            featureSettings[settingName] = key
            if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
                Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Value = key
                Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
            end
        end
        if control.Flag and Lumen.Flags[control.Flag] then
            Lumen.Flags[control.Flag].Set = control.Set
        end
    end
    return control
end

local function makePage(name, icon)
    local page = LumenWindow:Page({ Name = name, Icon = icon })
    local pageFrame = page.Frame
    local leftColumn = page.Left or (pageFrame and pageFrame:FindFirstChild("Left"))
    local rightColumn = page.Right or (pageFrame and pageFrame:FindFirstChild("Right"))
    assert(leftColumn and rightColumn, "MacLib page is missing its native left/right columns: " .. name)

    -- The scrolling frame is hosted inside Syllinse's animated body; columns stay native MacLib Frames.
    pageFrame.Name = "SyllinseMacLibPage_" .. name
    pageFrame.Parent = motionBody
    pageFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    pageFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(84, PANEL_TOP)
    pageFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 1, -(PANEL_TOP + FOOTER_HEIGHT))
    pageFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pageFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pageFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    pageFrame.Visible = false
    pageFrame.ScrollBarThickness = showLauncherButton and 10 or 3
    pageFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Dim
    pageFrame.ScrollBarImageTransparency = showLauncherButton and 0.15 or 0.35

    leftColumn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    leftColumn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    leftColumn.ClipsDescendants = false
    rightColumn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rightColumn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rightColumn.ClipsDescendants = false

    lumenPages[name] = page
    lumenHosts[name] = { Left = leftColumn, Right = rightColumn, Scroll = pageFrame }
    pageFrame:SetAttribute("SyllinseTabName", name)
    return page
end

    if targetPlayer ~= player and not table.find(selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name) then
        table.insert(selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name)
        priorityDropdown.UpdateOptions(selectedPlayerOptions)
    end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(targetPlayer)
    local index = table.find(selectedPlayerOptions, targetPlayer.Name)
    if index then
        table.remove(selectedPlayerOptions, index)
        priorityDropdown.UpdateOptions(selectedPlayerOptions)
    end
end)

-- ---------- Configs ----------
local ConfigList = pages.Configs:Section({ Name = "Config manager"; Side = "Left"; Icon = "folder" })
local ConfigActions = pages.Configs:Section({ Name = "Actions"; Side = "Right"; Icon = "save" })
ConfigList:Paragraph({
    Title = "MacLib config storage";
    Body = "Configs use MacLib's native flag serialization. File save/load requires the file APIs available in your client environment.";
})
local configNameInput = ConfigList:Input({
    Name = "Config name"; Value = "Syllinse"; Placeholder = "Type a config name";
    Callback = function() end;
})
local function cleanConfigNames(values)
    local clean = {}
    if type(values) == "table" then
        for _, name in ipairs(values) do
            if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
                table.insert(clean, name)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(clean)
    return clean
end

local configNames = cleanConfigNames(Lumen.ListConfigs())
local selectedConfig = ConfigList:Dropdown({
    Name = "Saved configs"; Options = configNames; Value = configNames[1] or ""; Search = true;
    Callback = function(value)
        if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then configNameInput.Set(value) end
    end;
})
local function refreshConfigs(preferredName, silent)
    local ok, options = pcall(function() return Lumen.ListConfigs() end)
    if not ok or type(options) ~= "table" then
        notify("Configs", "Could not list config files in this environment.", "Warning")
        return false
    end
    configNames = cleanConfigNames(options)
    selectedConfig.UpdateOptions(configNames)
    if type(preferredName) == "string" and table.find(configNames, preferredName) then
        selectedConfig.Set(preferredName)
    elseif #configNames == 0 then
        selectedConfig.Set("")
    end
    if not silent then notify("Configs", "Config list refreshed.", "Info", 1.5) end
    return true
end

local function getSelectedConfigName()
    local value = selectedConfig.Value
    if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" and table.find(configNames, value) then
        return value
    end
    -- Some clients expose read/write APIs but not listfiles; typed names remain usable there.
    if #configNames == 0 then
        local typed = configNameInput.Value
        if type(typed) == "string" then
            typed = typed:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if typed ~= "" then return typed end
        end
    end
    return ""
end
ConfigActions:Paragraph({
    Title = "Profile actions";
    Body = "Save or load all MacLib flags, including the control values in Combat, Desync, Visuals and Settings.";
})
ConfigActions:Label({
    Text = "Autoload config: checking...";
    Color = Theme.PanelText;
})

-- Native MacLib label rows are builder objects and do not expose a :Set() method.
-- Update the actual rendered TextLabel instead, so this status row cannot abort
-- the remaining UI construction if the library's wrapper API changes.
local function setAutoloadStatusText(text)
    local hosts = lumenHosts.Configs
    if not hosts then return false end
    for _, column in pairs(hosts) do
        if typeof(column) == "Instance" then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(column:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("TextLabel")
                    and string.find(descendant.Text, "^Autoload config:") then
                    descendant.Text = text
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Save config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = tostring(configNameInput.Value or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if name == "" then notify("Configs", "Enter a config name first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, saved = pcall(function() return Lumen.SaveConfig(name) end)
        if ok and saved then
            refreshConfigs(name, true)
            notify("Configs", "Saved '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else
            notify("Configs", "Save failed. Check file API support.", "Error")
        end
    end;
})
ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Load selected config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then notify("Configs", "Choose a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, loaded = pcall(function() return Lumen.LoadConfig(name) end)
        if ok and loaded then notify("Configs", "Loaded '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else notify("Configs", "Load failed or the file does not exist.", "Error") end
    end;
})
ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Delete selected config";
    Callback = function()
        local name = getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then notify("Configs", "Choose a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        local ok, deleted = pcall(function() return Lumen.DeleteConfig(name) end)
        if ok and deleted then refreshConfigs(nil, true); notify("Configs", "Deleted '" .. name .. "'.", "Success")
        else notify("Configs", "Delete failed or the file does not exist.", "Error") end
    end;
})
ConfigActions:Button({ Name = "Refresh config list"; Callback = refreshConfigs; })

-- The selected config name is stored separately so it can be loaded on the next launch.
local AUTOLOAD_FILE = "Syllinse_Autoload.txt"
local function getAutoloadName()
    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, AUTOLOAD_FILE)
    if not okExists or not exists then return nil end
    local okRead, value = pcall(readfile, AUTOLOAD_FILE)
    if okRead and type(value) == "string" then
        value = value:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        if value ~= "" then return value end
    end
    return nil
end
local function refreshAutoloadStatus()
    local name = getAutoloadName()
    local text = name and ("Autoload config: " .. name) or "Autoload config: None"
    -- Never let a cosmetic status refresh break the UI startup flow.
    local ok, updated = pcall(setAutoloadStatusText, text)
    if not ok or not updated then
        warn("[Syllinse] Could not refresh the autoload status label")
    end
end
refreshAutoloadStatus()
ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Set selected as autoload";
    Callback = function()
        local name = getSelectedConfigName()
        if name == "" then notify("Configs", "Choose or enter a saved config first.", "Warning"); return end
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
            notify("Configs", "Autoload requires file-write support in this client.", "Warning")
            return
        end
        local ok = pcall(writefile, AUTOLOAD_FILE, name)
        if ok then
            refreshAutoloadStatus()
            notify("Configs", "'" .. name .. "' will load on startup.", "Success")
        else
            notify("Configs", "Could not save the autoload selection.", "Error")
        end
    end;
})
ConfigActions:Button({
    Name = "Clear autoload";
    Callback = function()
        if type(delfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function" then
            notify("Configs", "Clearing autoload requires file-delete support.", "Warning")
            return
        end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if isfile(AUTOLOAD_FILE) then delfile(AUTOLOAD_FILE) end
        end)
        if ok then
            refreshAutoloadStatus()
            notify("Configs", "Autoload cleared.", "Info")
        else notify("Configs", "Could not clear autoload.", "Error") end
    end;
})

local terminologyLabelSetting = settingsLeft:Label({ Text = "Show Menu Function Terminology" })
addToggle(terminologyLabelSetting, {
    State = showMenuFunctionTerminology,
    Flag = "SyllinseMenuFunctionTerminology",
    Callback = function(state)
        showMenuFunctionTerminology = state == true
        featureSettings.ShowMenuFunctionTerminology = showMenuFunctionTerminology
        for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
            local label = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
            -- Keep the tab buttons' original position and hitbox in both modes.
            -- Labels remain independent of the clickable button; their UIScale
            -- mirrors the button's hover tween so the visual animation stays synced.
            if label then
                local targetY = nav.TerminologyLabelY or ((nav.BaseY or 122) + (nav.BaseIconSize or 64) + 25)
                if showMenuFunctionTerminology then
                    label.Visible = true
                    label.TextTransparency = 1
                    label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4)
                    tween(label, 0.16, {
                        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY),
                        TextTransparency = 0,
                    })
                else
                    tween(label, 0.12, {
                        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4),
                        TextTransparency = 1,
                    })
                    task.delay(0.13, function()
                        if label.Parent and not showMenuFunctionTerminology then
                            label.Visible = false
                            label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38.5, targetY + 4)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})
