-- A user-rebindable key hides only the Syllinse shell. MacLib's host remains enabled
-- so the watermark, keybind list, and notifications stay visible when the menu is hidden.
local uiToggleKey = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl
local uiToggleKeyListening = false
local uiToggleKeyLabel = settingsLeft:Label({ Text = "Toggle UI key" })
local uiToggleKeyControl = uiToggleKeyLabel:Keybind({
    Title = "Toggle UI";
    Key = uiToggleKey;
    Type = "Toggle";
    Flag = "SyllinseToggleUIKey";
    Callback = function() end;
})
if uiToggleKeyControl and uiToggleKeyControl.Key ~= nil then
    uiToggleKey = uiToggleKeyControl.Key
end
if uiToggleKeyControl and type(uiToggleKeyControl.Set) == "function" then
    local originalUiToggleKeySet = uiToggleKeyControl.Set
    uiToggleKeyControl.Set = function(key)
        originalUiToggleKeySet(key)
        uiToggleKey = key
    end
    if uiToggleKeyControl.Flag then
        Lumen.Flags[uiToggleKeyControl.Flag] = Lumen.Flags[uiToggleKeyControl.Flag] or {}
        Lumen.Flags[uiToggleKeyControl.Flag].Set = uiToggleKeyControl.Set
    end
    local keyButton = uiToggleKeyControl.Frame and uiToggleKeyControl.Frame:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextBox", true)
    if keyButton then
        keyButton.Focused:Connect(function()
            uiToggleKeyListening = true
        end)
    end
end
settingsLeft:Button({
    Name = "Test notification";
    Callback = function() notify("Settings", "Notification preferences are active.", "Success") end;
})

local applyPanelTypography

local function applyWindowColor(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Root = color
    root.BackgroundColor3 = color
    sidebar.BackgroundColor3 = color
    header.BackgroundColor3 = color
    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do nav.Button.BackgroundColor3 = color end
    Lumen.Theme.Background = color
    Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
end
local function applyPanelColor(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Surface = color
    searchFrame.BackgroundColor3 = color
    if searchLens and searchLens.Parent then searchLens.BackgroundColor3 = color end
    Lumen.Theme.Surface = color
    Lumen.Theme.SectionBorder = Theme.Stroke
    Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
end
applyPanelTypography = function(color)
    -- Use Syllinse's original neutral hierarchy: regular controls are muted gray,
    -- section headings slightly brighter, and helper text quieter. Keep the font sizes
    -- readable, but cap dropdown labels so they do not run into the selection field below.
    local controlText = color or Theme.PanelText
    local sectionTitle = Theme.PanelTitle
    local secondaryText = Theme.PanelSecondary
    local iconColor = Theme.PanelIcon
    local valueColor = Theme.PanelValue

    local function hasDropdownAncestor(object, sectionFrame)
        local current = object.Parent
        for _ = 1, 5 do
            if not current or current == sectionFrame then break end
            local ancestorName = string.lower(current.Name or "")
            if ancestorName:find("dropdown", 1, true)
                or ancestorName:find("drop-down", 1, true)
                or ancestorName == "inputframe"
                or ancestorName == "buttonframe" then
                return true
            end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function makeMobileRowTappable(control, sectionFrame, physicalHeight)
        -- Keep the library's original row and control geometry on touch devices too.
        return
    end

    for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
        local frame = section.Frame
        if frame and frame.Parent then
            for _, descendant in ipairs(frame:GetDescendants()) do
                local name = string.lower(descendant.Name or "")
                local inDropdown = hasDropdownAncestor(descendant, frame)

                if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                    local targetColor = controlText
                    local isSectionTitle = descendant.Name == "Title"
                        and descendant.Parent
                        and descendant.Parent.Name == "Header"
                    local isSecondary = name:find("body", 1, true)
                        or name:find("description", 1, true)
                        or name:find("sublabel", 1, true)
                        or name:find("placeholder", 1, true)
                    local isValue = name == "amount" or name:find("value", 1, true)

                    if isSectionTitle then
                        targetColor = sectionTitle
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_MEDIUM
                        descendant.TextSize = math.clamp(descendant.TextSize, 15, 16)
                        -- Shift only the section title text down by 1 px without moving the icon or divider.
                        -- PaddingTop=2 moves the centered text content down by 1 px and is reused on refresh.
                        local titleYOffset = descendant:FindFirstChild("SyllinseTitleYOffset")
                        if not titleYOffset then
                            titleYOffset = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                            titleYOffset.Name = "SyllinseTitleYOffset"
                            titleYOffset.Parent = descendant
                        end
                        titleYOffset.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 0)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 0)
                        titleYOffset.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 0)
                    elseif isSecondary then
                        targetColor = secondaryText
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                        descendant.TextSize = math.clamp(descendant.TextSize, 13, 14)
                    elseif isValue then
                        targetColor = valueColor
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                        descendant.TextSize = math.clamp(descendant.TextSize, 14, 15)
                    else
                        descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                        descendant.TextSize = math.clamp(descendant.TextSize, 14, 15)
                    end

                    -- Label text above dropdown fields is the cramped case. A 14 px cap
                    -- keeps those labels inside their original row without moving any UI.
                    if inDropdown and not isValue then
                        descendant.TextSize = math.min(descendant.TextSize, 14)
                    end

                    if inDropdown and (name == "input" or isValue) then
                        targetColor = Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223)
                    end
                    descendant.TextColor3 = targetColor
                    if descendant:IsA("TextBox") then
                        descendant.PlaceholderColor3 = secondaryText
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceHover
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
                    descendant.FontFace = FONT_REGULAR
                    -- Give mobile users a taller row for switches and keybind/action controls.
                    -- The row grows with the target so it does not overlap the control below it.
                    if descendant.Name == "Toggle" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 30)
                    elseif descendant.Name == "KeyButton" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 30)
                    elseif descendant.Name == "Click" then
                        makeMobileRowTappable(descendant, frame, 32)
                    end
                    if descendant.Name ~= "Click" then
                        if inDropdown and descendant.Name == "Input" then
                            -- Selected values such as Head and Closest are brighter than helper labels.
                            descendant.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223)
                            descendant.TextTransparency = 0
                        else
                            descendant.TextColor3 = controlText
                        end
                        descendant.TextSize = math.clamp(descendant.TextSize, 14, 15)
                        -- Keep dropdown text legible but within its original field.
                        if inDropdown then
                            descendant.TextSize = math.min(descendant.TextSize, 15)
                        end
                    else
                        descendant.TextSize = 13
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") or descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
                    if name == "sliderbar" then
                        descendant.ImageColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
                    else
                        descendant.ImageColor3 = iconColor
                    end
                elseif descendant:IsA("Frame") then
                    if name == "button" and descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name == "SliderFrame" then
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderTrack
                    elseif name == "circle" then
                        descendant.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SliderHandle
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- Keep Lumen popups and the overlay on MacLib's native BuilderSans family too.
-- Use FontFace + explicit weights so letterforms are consistent across all UI layers.
local function applyMacLibTypeface(instance)
    if not instance then return end
    local function styleText(object)
        if not (object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox")) then
            return
        end
        local name = string.lower(object.Name or "")
        local parentName = object.Parent and string.lower(object.Parent.Name or "") or ""
        local medium = (name == "title" and parentName == "header")
            or name == "toasttitle"
            or name == "notificationtitle"
            or name == "paragraphheader"
            or name == "headertext"
        object.FontFace = medium and FONT_MEDIUM or FONT_REGULAR
        object.TextScaled = false
        object.AutoLocalize = false
    end
    styleText(instance)
    for _, descendant in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
        styleText(descendant)
    end
end

local function connectMacLibTypeface(rootObject)
    if not rootObject then return end
    applyMacLibTypeface(rootObject)
    rootObject.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
        task.defer(function()
            if descendant and descendant.Parent then
                applyMacLibTypeface(descendant)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

connectMacLibTypeface(root)
connectMacLibTypeface(Lumen and Lumen._Instance)
connectMacLibTypeface(Lumen and Lumen._Overlay)

local function applyTextColor(color, panelColor)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Text = color
    Theme.PanelText = panelColor or color
    headerTitle.TextColor3 = Theme.PanelTitle
    searchBox.TextColor3 = color
    Lumen.Theme.Text = Theme.PanelText
    Lumen.Theme.TextDim = Theme.PanelIcon
    Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
end
local function applyAccentColor(color)
    if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then return end
    Theme.Accent = color
    Theme.IconActive = color
    Lumen.Theme.Accent = color
    Lumen.ApplyTheme()
    applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)
    for _, nav in ipairs(navButtons) do
        local selected = nav.Name == activeName
        if nav.Icon then nav.Icon.ImageColor3 = selected and color or Theme.IconIdle end
        local label = terminologyLabels[nav.Name]
        if label then
            local labelColor = selected and terminologyTint(Theme.IconActive, 0.76)
                or (nav.Hovering and terminologyTint(Theme.IconHover, 0.86) or Theme.IconIdle)
            tween(label, 0.14, { TextColor3 = labelColor })
        end
        setFallbackEmphasis(nav.Button, selected)
        if nav.Gear then setGearIconColor(nav.Gear, selected and color or Theme.IconIdle) end
    end
    for tabName, entry in pairs(headerIcons) do
        local selected = tabName == activeName
        -- Keep the title-row icon matched to its neutral gray title, even if the user
        -- changes the separate sidebar accent color in Settings.
        if entry.Icon then entry.Icon.ImageColor3 = selected and Theme.HeaderIconTint or Theme.PanelIcon end
        recolorFallback(
            entry.Holder:FindFirstChild("ImageFallback"),
            selected,
            Theme.PanelTitle,
            Theme.PanelIcon
        )
    end
    if headerGear then setGearIconColor(headerGear, color) end
end
settingsRight:Label({ Text = "Window background" }):Colorpicker({
    Title = "Window background"; Color = Theme.Root; Flag = "ThemeWindow";
    Callback = function(color) applyWindowColor(color) end;
})
settingsRight:Label({ Text = "Panel surface" }):Colorpicker({
    Title = "Panel surface"; Color = Theme.Surface; Flag = "ThemePanels";
    Callback = function(color) applyPanelColor(color) end;
})
settingsRight:Label({ Text = "Text color" }):Colorpicker({
    Title = "Text color"; Color = Theme.Text; Flag = "ThemeText";
    Callback = function(color) applyTextColor(color) end;
})
settingsRight:Label({ Text = "Accent color" }):Colorpicker({
    Title = "Accent color"; Color = Theme.Accent; Flag = "ThemeAccent";
    Callback = function(color) applyAccentColor(color) end;
})
settingsRight:Button({
    Name = "Reset theme";
    Callback = function()
        applyWindowColor(Color3.fromRGB(23, 22, 22))
        applyPanelColor(Color3.fromRGB(26, 25, 25))
        applyTextColor(Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223), Color3.fromRGB(139, 137, 137))
        applyAccentColor(Color3.fromRGB(224, 223, 223))
        notify("Settings", "Theme reset.", "Success")
    end;
})
settingsRight:Paragraph({ Title = "About"; Body = "Syllinse keeps its original icon rail and header. MacLib supplies native section cards, widgets, popups and config serialization; section panels stay in their assigned columns." })

-- Apply theme links once more after every page/section/control has been constructed.
-- Lumen registers many native widget surfaces lazily as each control is created.
Lumen.ApplyTheme()

applyPanelTypography(Theme.PanelText)

-- Live feature search across MacLib controls registered by the Syllinse adapter. Category prefixes
-- navigate to the matching Syllinse tab; all other terms filter Lumen rows in place.
local function startsWith(text, prefix)
    return text:sub(1, #prefix) == prefix
end
local function filterLumenControls(query)
    query = string.lower(query or "")
    local sectionMatches = {}
    local pageMatches = {}

    if query == "" then
        for _, item in ipairs(Lumen.Searchable or {}) do
            if item.Frame and item.Frame.Parent then item.Frame.Visible = true end
        end
        for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
            if section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then section.Frame.Visible = true end
        end
        return
    end

    for _, section in ipairs(Lumen.Sections or {}) do
        local matched = string.find(string.lower(tostring(section.Name or "")), query, 1, true) ~= nil
        sectionMatches[section] = matched
        if section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then section.Frame.Visible = matched end
        if matched and section.PageFrame then
            local tabName = section.PageFrame:GetAttribute("SyllinseTabName")
            if tabName then pageMatches[tabName] = true end
        end
    end

    for _, item in ipairs(Lumen.Searchable or {}) do
        local frame = item.Frame
        if frame and frame.Parent then
            local section = item.Section
            local text = string.lower(tostring(item.Text or ""))
            local match = string.find(text, query, 1, true) ~= nil or (section and sectionMatches[section] == true)
            frame.Visible = match
            if match and section and section.Frame and section.Frame.Parent then
                section.Frame.Visible = true
            end
            if match and section and section.PageFrame then
                local tabName = section.PageFrame:GetAttribute("SyllinseTabName")
                if tabName then pageMatches[tabName] = true end
            end
        end
    end

    local currentHasMatch = pageMatches[activeName] == true
    if not currentHasMatch then
        for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
            if pageMatches[item.Name] then
                setActiveTab(item.Name)
                break
            end
        end
    end
end

searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local query = string.lower((searchBox.Text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or "")
    if query == "" then
        currentSearchMode = "none"
        filterLumenControls("")
        return
    end

    local tabPrefix
    for _, item in ipairs(navItems) do
        local name = string.lower(item.Name)
        if startsWith(name, query) then
            tabPrefix = item.Name
            break
        end
    end
    if tabPrefix then
        currentSearchMode = "tab"
        filterLumenControls("")
        if activeName ~= tabPrefix then setActiveTab(tabPrefix) end
    else
        currentSearchMode = "controls"
        filterLumenControls(query)
    end
end)

-- Export the native library, page/section handles and settings so feature code can
-- attach to the same native Lumen controls without using a second widget library.
local SyllinseEnvironment = _G
if type(getgenv) == "function" then
    local ok, environment = pcall(getgenv)
    if ok and type(environment) == "table" then SyllinseEnvironment = environment end
elseif type(shared) == "table" then
    SyllinseEnvironment = shared
end
SyllinseEnvironment.Lumen = Lumen
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseMacLibWindow = LumenWindow
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseMacLibPages = pages
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseTabs = lumenPages
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseFeatureSettings = featureSettings
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseRoot = root
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseScreenGui = gui
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseNotify = notify
SyllinseEnvironment.SyllinseSetFeature = function(flag, value)
    if type(flag) ~= "string" then return false end
    local ok = pcall(function() Lumen.SetFlag(flag, value) end)
    return ok
end

-- Correct initial tab visibility after all native sections have been mounted.
for name, page in pairs(lumenPages) do
    if page.Frame then page.Frame.Visible = false end
end
setActiveTab("Combat")
-- Load the chosen profile after all controls/flags are registered.
local startupAutoload = getAutoloadName()
if startupAutoload then
    local ok, loaded = pcall(function() return Lumen.LoadConfig(startupAutoload) end)
    if ok and loaded then
        notify("Configs", "Autoloaded '" .. startupAutoload .. "'.", "Success", 2)
    else
        notify("Configs", "Autoload failed for '" .. startupAutoload .. "'.", "Warning", 2)
    end
end
notify("Syllinse", "MacLib controls loaded.", "Success", 2)

-- Put every shell visual inside one CanvasGroup so the same grouped fade used by
-- Magik Society can run independently of the outer window's background morph.
-- UIScale and the root's own corner/stroke stay on the outer frame.
for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
    if child ~= motionBody and child:IsA("GuiObject") then
        child.Parent = motionBody
    end
end
motionBody.GroupTransparency = 1
refreshLauncherVisibility()

-- Open by morphing from the Syllinse launcher pill into the full window.
setWindowOpen(true)

-- Toggle the Syllinse main window with the user-configured key. Keep Lumen's
-- backend/overlay ScreenGuis enabled so watermark, keybind list, and toasts stay visible.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    -- The next input after clicking the key selector is the new key; don't toggle the UI
    -- while Lumen is capturing that input for rebinding.
    if uiToggleKeyListening then
        uiToggleKeyListening = false
        return
    end
    if gameProcessed then return end

    local matchesToggleKey = input.KeyCode == uiToggleKey or input.UserInputType == uiToggleKey
    if matchesToggleKey then
        setWindowOpen(not windowIsOpen)
    end
end)

if playerGui:GetAttribute("SyllinseReadyLogShown") ~= true then
    playerGui:SetAttribute("SyllinseReadyLogShown", true)
    print("[Syllinse] MacLib integration ready")
end
